**Done items leave this file (Q16, 2026-09-26):** a ticked `- [x]` item is moved
verbatim to the dated archive docs/archive/OPEN-done-YYYY-MM.md by
`npm run inventories:refresh` (scripts/archive-done.mjs); check:generated fails
while one is left here. The queue score, next free number and the done-item
guard check read this file AND the archives, so archiving never changes a count.

**`[~]` items say when they are done (2026-09-27):** put what is left as one or
more markers on the item, written out in full and ANDed together:
``done-when: sql `SELECT <one column>` => <expected>`` (read-only, first row),
`done-when: test <path>`, `done-when: issue #N closed`, or `done-when: pr #N merged`.
Only add markers when they cover everything that is left (a screenshot or a
review still to do means no marker yet). `node scripts/open-done-when.mjs` runs them;
the nightly `open-done-when` workflow opens one nightly-red issue listing the items
that are ready to tick. src/test/openPartlyDoneItemsSayDoneWhen.test.ts ratchets the
count of `[~]` items with no marker.

   token (403 on reading issues), so the alert ledger can't sync Sentry. You
   said "you can do it", but creating and copying an API token is a credential
   step I'm not allowed to do. It takes about 2 minutes: Sentry -> Settings ->
   Auth Tokens (or Custom Integrations) -> new token with `project:read` +
   `event:read` -> `gh secret set SENTRY_READ_TOKEN`. I'll wire the sync to
   that name, so it closes the "Sentry not synced" ledger item on the next hourly run.

4. **Should seed/test accounts receive real email? (Q2/Q29)** Every dead
   **ANSWERED 2026-09-23 (owner pop-up): keep sending to test accounts until launch; stop at launch (on the launch checklist as Q144).**
   letter so far went to a test account, and the cause was Resend's DAILY
   QUOTA: the 2026-09-13 seed-heavy run enqueued 319 emails in one day. Test
   mail spends the same quota real users' sign-in and receipt mail needs. The
   option: skip sending to is_seed recipients (log it as `skipped_seed` in

   Your call, because it changes what the E2E journeys can check.
5. **Push notifications (Q82): three things only you can do.** The code
   **ANSWERED 2026-09-26 (owner pop-up): "cut it when main is current".** Claude runs fastlane beta from main
   once the open landings are in and main CI is green; you install it and open the app once.
   cause is fixed and pushed (see Q82), but no phone has the fix yet.
   (a) **Cut a TestFlight build from main** (at or after the Q82 fix commit)
   and install it on your iPhone. The only builds with the AppDelegate
   forwarding are ce4d38d59 (2026-09-02) and later, and every build so far
   also has the boot race (it depends on timing, so it may not hit every
   launch). (b) **On that build: sign in, go to Home, tap "Enable" on the
   notifications pill, then Allow.** The pill only shows after you've posted
   or applied, or on a later launch more than an hour after install. If you tapped "Don't
   Allow" on any earlier build, iOS will not ask again: turn it on in
   Settings > Helpr > Notifications first, then cold-launch the app. Within
   seconds `select count(*) from push_tokens` should be 1 and
   analytics_events should show `push_token_saved`. If it doesn't, the
   analytics events say which step failed. (c) **Only if you run the app
   straight from Xcode on your phone:** those builds get SANDBOX APNs tokens,
   and prod sends to the production APNs host (`APNS_USE_SANDBOX` is unset),
   so APNs rejects them with BadDeviceToken and the sender deletes the row.
   TestFlight/App Store builds are unaffected. Leave this alone unless you
   test from Xcode. APNs credentials are fine: APNS_KEY_ID, APNS_TEAM_ID
   (digest matches P85MCK558V), APNS_BUNDLE_ID (digest matches com.Helpr)
   and APNS_AUTH_KEY are all set in Supabase secrets. Whether the .p8 key
   itself is valid can't be checked without a real token to send to.

7. **Turn off Supabase's GitHub auto-deploy to production (Q121). Top priority.**
   On every push to main, Supabase's GitHub integration applies new migrations
   (and deploys edge functions) to prod within about a minute. Our deploy
   workflow's safety checks (lint, replay test, destructive-DDL stop) run after
   that, so for the last 24h (24 migrations) they have not blocked anything.
   Measured from prod's own logs 2026-09-23 (Q117). What you do: Supabase
   dashboard > Project Settings > Integrations > GitHub, and switch off
   automatic deploys to the production branch (exact toggle name not verified).
   Until then, each new migration turns db-deploy red by design (the new
   provenance check), so the side channel stays visible.
   **ANSWERED 2026-09-26 (owner asked "whats the best option?"): switch it OFF.** Measured: db-deploy
   36261725456 (18:16Z) judged 124 prod versions since the cutoff, 108 with a db-deploy receipt and 16
   acknowledged side-channel applies, the newest of them 2026-09-23 ~16:10Z (Q194). So nothing has come
   in by the side channel for about 3 days, but that is not proof the toggle is off. Off is the only
   setting where db-deploy's lint, replay and destructive-DDL checks actually run before prod changes.
   Still the owner's dashboard click; the provenance check stays as the detector either way.

7. **Backups (Q45): the restore is proven weekly now; five things are yours.**
   **ANSWERED 2026-09-23 (owner pop-ups): (a) yes, Pro is paid and intended; (b) keep 14-day retention; (c) back up uploaded files EXCEPT id-documents, encrypted (work item Q147); (d) no PITR (no paid upgrade); (e) BACKUP_PASSPHRASE and ban_fingerprint_salt go in the owner's password manager (owner to-do).**
   Measured 2026-09-23. Nothing below is needed for the drill to stay green.
   (a) **Is the Supabase org really on Pro?** The API says `plan: "pro"`, and
   `supabase backups list` shows 7 daily platform backups. Every doc said
   "free tier". If you are paying $25/mo, you already have in-place daily
   restores for 7 days. If you did not mean to be on Pro, that is a bill.
   (b) **Backup retention is 14 days, not 90.** The repo caps artifacts and
   logs at 14 days (`maximum_allowed_days` 90). Raising it to 90 is one
   setting, but it lengthens EVERY workflow's artifacts, and on the free
   GitHub plan that can go past the artifact-storage quota (billed). The
   alternative is a monthly copy somewhere else. Your call.
   (c) **Storage files (photos, ID documents) are in NO backup**: 152 objects,
   about 10 MB (proof-photos 64, job-photos 61, message-attachments 18,
   user-documents 4, avatars 4, id-documents 1). The platform backup excludes
   them too. Backing them up needs a service-role key as an Actions secret and
   puts ID documents (encrypted) into a GitHub artifact: a privacy trade-off.
   (d) **PITR** (restore to the minute) is a paid add-on, about $100/mo plus
   Small compute. Without it, worst-case loss is about 24 h (platform) or
   24 h + GitHub's up-to-7 h schedule delay (ours). You said no paid upgrades,
   so this is only the measured gap.
   (e) **Two secrets that no backup holds.** `BACKUP_PASSPHRASE`: is there a
   copy outside GitHub? If not, losing the GitHub account also loses every
   backup. `ban_fingerprint_salt` (vault): it cannot be re-created, and
   without it every stored ban fingerprint stops matching after a restore.
   Want it (encrypted) in the backup, or kept in your password manager?

29. **PR #1810 (Q392): which parish job-match alert design stays? (2026-09-26).** PR #1810 and main
    (20260926041132, Q225/V-008) both fix "parish alert arrives before early access shows the job", differently:
    (A) KEEP MAIN'S queue-only design (every send goes through the 1-minute sweep, so elite/no-delay users hear up
    to ~60s later; nothing sent inside the funding write) and ADD the PR's block check (`user_blocks`, both ways)
    and shared `job_announceable_to()` gate. A user who deletes the alert can be told again if the job reopens.
    (B) TAKE THE PR'S design: elite/no-delay users told inline at funding (incl. email via net.http_post inside the
    escrow write), a permanent per-(user, job) `job_match_queue` ledger so nobody is ever told twice, block check;
    main's `parish_match_alert_queue` and its sweep step are retired.
    Measured live (pg_get_functiondef, prod): neither `notify_helpers_on_job_post` nor `deliver_parish_match_alert`
    checks `user_blocks` today; `deliver_job_match`/`job_announceable_to` do not exist on prod.
    Not blocked by this: the PR's instant-match half, digest gating, export_my_data line, and Q401 (auth-page
    noindex) are being landed separately. The PR's old-timestamp migrations must never reach main as-is.
    **ANSWERED 2026-09-26 (owner pop-up): "A + never twice": keep main's queue-only send path, add the
    `user_blocks` check (both ways) and `job_announceable_to()` gate, AND write parish sends into the permanent
    per-(user, job) ledger so a deleted alert is never re-sent when the job reopens. Lands after the instant half.**
    **DONE 2026-09-26: landed as migration 20260926195608 (Q392 parish half).**
30. **press-every-control: press one representative per repeated list row? (#1582, 2026-09-26).** Run
    36230166945: /admin?view=jobs alone took 97 min (412 presses), mostly the same four controls on every job
    card. Landed without asking: shards now claim rows from one shared queue (Q725), so a heavy row no longer
    strands the rest of its shard. Still yours: (A) keep pressing every card (complete, slow; ~97 min a run on
    that view), or (B) press every control on the first N cards of a repeated list and only count the rest
    (much faster; a defect that shows only on card N+1 would be missed). Default until answered: (A).
31. **Group crews: three money rules (Q728/Q729, 2026-09-27).** Group jobs are off
    (GROUP_JOBS_ENABLED=false), so nothing is charged under these yet. (A) CREW_FEE_PAYS_UNCONFIRMED:
    a late-cancel fee is shared by a member the poster OFFERED the job to but who never confirmed.
    Built as yes (your Q407(13) answer); confirm. (B) CREW_COMPLETES_WHEN_HIRED_DONE: an under-filled
    crew completes when every hired member is done, and the unfilled shares go back to the poster.
    Built as yes; confirm. (C) New: when a crew dispute or an unfilled slot returns a member's share,
    only the share itself (budget + urgent fee) goes back. The platform fee, sales tax and any gift
    card on that charge are NOT returned. Should they be returned in proportion to the share?
    ANSWERED 2026-09-27: yes, pro-rata. BUILT (see the ANSWERED block above); guard src/test/edge/process-scheduled-payouts-crew.test.ts.

## FEEDS — mirrored from the alert ledger, nightly-red issues and the audit bus (node scripts/open-sync-trackers.mjs)

Each item mirrors one open problem; its `feed:` tags are sticky (never edit them) and its `done-when:` holds once the source closes, when the sync flips it to `[~]`. The same problem seen again while its item is open is attached to that item (marked Seen again), never filed twice; every red overnight check is one sub-line of the ONE Fix the test robots item, which the sync rewrites.

- [~] **Q865 MEDIUM** (nightly-red #1754 prod-audit, 2026-09-30): 156 of run 36766443019's 158 skips are NOT justified. deep-links, messy-input and interruptions call `ensureFundedOpenJob` in `beforeAll`; under live Stripe it calls `skipLivePay` (`test.skip`), which in a `beforeAll` skips EVERY test in the file, including those that never touch the funded job (signed-out redirects, gone-job, /user/:id, the six /profile?tab= links). Only tests that read `fx.openJob` should skip; they already carry per-test `test.skip(!fx.openJob, …)`. The other 2 skips (expanding-search) are justified. Fix: let the fixture return "none" in live mode instead of skipping, keep the live-pay skip per test, and GUARD the class: a `beforeAll` must not call a fixture that can `test.skip` (static scan of e2e/prod-audit, @mutate one spec back). Then re-measure `prod-audit.perTest`: set to 138.2 (the max of runs 36744778439 = 138.2 and 36766443019 = 137.7, both with 91 tests run) so the budget step passes today. Run 36364639347 (before the switch, 124 tests) measured 109.9, which is inside the two-way band, so the un-skip will not trip it; lower it to the new measurement in the same commit. done-when: issue #2245 closed (tick when a prod-audit run shows only justified skips, and `prod-audit.perTest` equals its measured value). **Fixed 2026-09-30 (Q865 lane):** the three beforeAll hooks now mint inside `unlessLivePay(() => ...)` (e2e/prod-audit/fundedOpenJob.ts), which turns the live-pay skip into a value; only tests that open a funded fixture skip, per test, via `skipWhenUnfundedLive` -> `skipLivePay`. Guard: `src/test/journeysNeverPayLive.test.ts` "no beforeAll mints a funded fixture outside unlessLivePay" (2 @mutate lines, both killed). **perTest NOT changed:** the un-skipped tests now run, so `prod-audit.perTest` must be RE-MEASURED from the next real prod-audit run and set to that value (not 138.2, which was measured with 91 tests). Same mechanism as the owner's 10-09 21:45Z incident (commit 60de96aeb): getSession() returns null when the stored access token has expired and the refresh fails, and supabase-js then sends the anon key. 60de96aeb/63dc73e2a/24bed73f1 (landed 2026-10-11, not in either failing release d9342ecf/c123c618) stop the false sign-out but NOT the anon requests. Since 2026-10-10 14:39Z: 0 events (Sentry `message:*permission denied for*`, 7d, read 04:2xZ). The iOS burst 10-09 13:38-13:40Z ran 2.5 min across /home, /post-job, /profile, /messages, /jobs, so it is not only a sub-second wake race. Fix still to build (an auth change, so it carries a Sensitive check trailer): before a /rest/v1 or /functions/v1 request leaves with the anon bearer while auth-js still holds a session that failed to refresh on the network, wait for TOKEN_REFRESHED (auth-js caches a failed refresh for REFRESH_FAILURE_COOLDOWN_MS = 60 s, so a retry inside that window gets the cached failure) and send with the user's token, else send as today; guard on the real supabase-js client like src/lib/authSignOut.lateLogout.test.ts. **ONE ROOT CAUSE, MERGED 2026-10-14** (owner: items that share one root cause are one item). Merged here: Q1597 (dashboard.ctx applications), Q1598 (backout_notices), Q1600 (job_schedule_change_requests), Q1601 (get_job_offer_targets), Q1602 (applications), Q1603 (jobs), Q1609 (referral_codes), Q1620 (get_my_saved_helpers), Q1630 (login_history), Q1811 (profiles again, new bundle hash), Q1621 (missing authorization header), Q1636 and Q1812 (session lost on a signed-in screen), Q1610 and Q1700 (functionshttperror, one Sentry issue JAVASCRIPT-1Q), Q1599 and Q1584 (error screen "we couldn't load this"); recurring_visit_payments, payout_transfers and gift_cards also fired in the same bursts. MEASURED 2026-10-14: (1) Sentry, 100 newest "permission denied for" events (14 d): 98 on iOS with NO Sentry user (src/main.tsx sets the user from the Supabase session, so no user = no session), on signed-in routes (/home, /posts, /jobs, /profile, /post-job, /messages), in bursts 2026-10-09 01:38Z, 02:48Z, 13:37-13:40Z, then 10-10 02:51Z/12:00Z/14:39Z and 10-13 22:57Z/23:01Z (profiles only); 2 on Mac OS with a user (10-09 21:45Z). (2) The "session lost on a signed-in screen" events (JAVASCRIPT-3H, iOS, no user) carry the SAME timestamps as the 10-10 and 10-13 profiles events; "missing authorization header" (JAVASCRIPT-35) and the real functionshttperror events (JAVASCRIPT-1Q, 10-09 02:48Z and 13:38Z, iOS, no user) sit inside the bursts (1Q's 10-12 events are automated, the test poster). The error-screen ledger rows for /posts and the Sentry "we couldn't load this" rows were first and last seen inside the same bursts. (3) Grants on prod: every table/function named is granted to `authenticated` and NOT to `anon` (applications, jobs: column SELECT for authenticated only; backout_notices, job_schedule_change_requests, referral_codes, login_history, recurring_visit_payments, payout_transfers, gift_cards: table SELECT for authenticated only; get_job_offer_targets, get_my_saved_helpers: EXECUTE for authenticated only; profiles: anon may SELECT but only authenticated may UPDATE, 87 columns). So the queries ran as anon. Then an admin taps Mark Reviewed on it in Admin > Stuck Jobs (resolve_stalled_job_flag): that is the ONLY writer of job_completion_nudges.resolved_at (measured in pg_proc), and both alerts' close rule (admin_queue_still_pending 'stalled-job') waits for it, not for the job's status.

(archive L2133) **OWNER DECIDED 2026-10-04 (pop-up, with Q1076): tests get ONE permanent test-only paid is_seed job set in the database (no real money, never shown to real users, excluded from payout jobs).** **SCOPED 2026-10-05 (alerts lane): this build is now the root cause of nightly-red #2245 (prod-audit, 21 of 25 failures), #2213 and #2294 (Q722 empty-account shapes), part of #2198, and so #2244.** The states the suites need between poster-e2e and helper-e2e: an open escrow job the Helpr has not applied to; accepted and confirmed but not on the way; on the way / in_progress; completed with no review; completed with a review the Helpr can answer; an unanswered dispute against the Helpr. The hard part is money: is_seed alone does not keep these rows away from auto-release-payment, process-scheduled-payouts, void-cancelled-payments, auto-resolve-disputes (a durable dispute would be auto-resolved and try a Stripe refund), charge-recurring-visits, auto-expire-jobs and money-reconciliation. That needs a dedicated fixture marker that every money cron and the reconciliation skip, a guard that derives the money crons and requires the skip, and an lh-money-escrow review. It is too large to start at the end of this session. Next: build it as one money-reviewed lane. **MERGED IN 2026-10-06 (triage):** Q941 (the 7 interruptions.spec.ts apply tests skip until the open un-applied escrow job exists; prod-audit.yml runs no seeder; still 7 skips on run 37270491218) and Q1076 (seed producers bypass accept_job/respond_to_direct_offer and leave states real jobs never reach; per the same 2026-10-04 decision they stamp states realistically). **MERGED IN 2026-10-06 (triage, second pass):** Q856 (shell-spacing reports messages@320-430 UNMEASURED until poster-e2e has two Active threads on live jobs). Q593 (a11y-prod's RESERVED accepted fixture) is the same build but stays its own item: it now mirrors nightly-red #2375. **DESIGN 2026-10-06 (e2e-reds lane; owner re-confirmed ONE permanent paid is_seed job, never public; lead: post-launch, both reviews before landing):** measured on prod 2026-10-06: anon open_jobs_browse = [] and 0 open/accepted/in_progress jobs with payment_status escrow (119 open abandoned seeds, 1 open unpaid, 1 accepted unpaid), so a11y-prod job-detail-1 skips UNJUSTIFIED (6 in run 37403676136) and prod-audit 37521973791 fails 16 tests + 7 unjustified skips on 'no in-progress job between the shared accounts'. (a) VISIBILITY: seed_jobs_hidden_publicly() is false on prod (feature_flags key unset), so an escrow seed would show on public Browse today (prod-seed.mjs:534 refuses escrow seeds for this reason), and flipping it true would also hide the row from helper-e2e. Migration: seed jobs visible only to viewers whose profiles.is_seed is true, on all four browse surfaces (open_jobs_browse, get_ranked_open_jobs, get_open_jobs_for_map, get_public_open_jobs): lh-authz-rls review. (b) UNHIREABLE: an escrow row with no PaymentIntent that press-every-control or a journey could apply to, hire and release would make release-payout transfer from the LIVE platform balance (no source_transaction). A fixture marker that apply_to_job / accept_application / release paths and every money cron listed above refuse, plus a guard deriving the money crons: lh-money-escrow review. (c) a service-role ensure script run by each workflow's fixtures job; a11y-prod authedScreens and the prod-audit gap lookups read the fixture id instead of open_jobs_browse. **BUILT 2026-10-07 (proof lane, owner decision 2026-10-07: ONE durable is_seed job funded in the database only, visible only to the test accounts, applied to and withdrawn from by 01-browse):** migration 20261007122020 adds public.test_fixture_jobs (server-only) and trg_jobs_test_fixture_never_hired, which for a listed job refuses, for every role, a Helpr, a direct offer, any status but open/cancelled and a payment_status move while open (PGlite src/test/pglite/testFixtureJobsCannotBeHired.pglite.mjs: 12 FAILED without it, ALL PASS with it, 3x replay); scripts/e2e/browseFixture.mjs keeps exactly one (ends an unusable open one before unregistering it); e2e/journeys/01-browse.spec.ts ensures it, applies, withdraws and cleans up in a finally. Guard: src/test/testFixtureJobsCannotBeHired.test.ts (6 @mutate, each red). lh-money-escrow review: SAFE WITH FIXES, both closed: (1) an unusable open fixture is ended before it is unregistered; (2) CI's poster is poster-e2e (71c56dfb), measured read-only: all 28 '[E2E DO NOT ACCEPT]' jobs created inside e2e-journeys 37552757280 + 37554735234's windows (00:35-01:47Z) and all 42 since 00:34Z are owned by 71c56dfb, none by 96c9899e; locally getSession('poster') mints poster-e2e too, so CI and local keep the same fixture. Still open in this item: the other suite states (accepted, in progress, completed, dispute: prod-audit / a11y / press), the /jobs:id + /user:id stall, press self-heal and the push 'Not Now' signal. **STATE FIXTURES BUILT 2026-10-12 (lane seed-fixtures, branch ready/seed-fixtures), applied to prod the same day:** scripts/e2e/seedStateFixtures.mjs (`--apply` idempotent, `--verify` read-only, `--teardown`, `--only=<key>`) keeps these poster-e2e jobs, is_seed, funded in the database only (escrow, no PaymentIntent, no Checkout Session), helper-e2e hired through the app's own RPCs: helper-scheduled-4d a87934d0-bb91-5215-b509-d49744cb2c5a and helper-scheduled-daybefore 9cd3fa38-7e53-57ca-813b-71bc15c8e389 (accepted; each with a 2-message thread; for Q1554, Q1561, Q593, Q1427), helper-arrived-photo 90aa6c8b-bf01-5430-ab29-ac182087ad5e (photo required, GPS-verified arrival, no before photo; Q1562), helper-completed c975aec8-ef5a-5fca-bac1-237320e7e557 (completed, payment_status released in the DB only; Q1561, work record), dispute-refiled a690c95f-ecd8-5791-86ce-b21b0c797674 (disputed, Helpr answered, withdrawn, re-filed; Q1327), crew-unconfirmed 0f2f7753-9390-57d1-8c4c-fcc84259bad3 (booked crew of helper-e2e + eli, neither confirmed; Q1402), and wipe row 2edc2b13-de6b-5d52-9528-c5fc168e4d81 in pre_verification_wipes for the incomplete account 60416646 (storage_done_at set, so the wipe sweep skips it; Q1286). Every child row id (applications, disputes, roster, messages) is printed by the script. NOT listed in test_fixture_jobs (its trigger refuses every status move, and a refusal inside a cron's set-based sweep would roll the sweep back for real users); the money safety is that every money mover refuses a job with no PaymentIntent before calling Stripe, the money crons skip is_seed, and no test account has a Stripe account. prod-seed.mjs never reviews or disputes them. Ages: the scheduled pair and the crew's 48 h answer window; re-run `--apply` to restore. NOT built: Q1326's two overlapping hires (no double-booking rule on main yet). Guard: src/test/seedStateFixtures.test.ts.

So prod GoTrue has CAPTCHA ON for password sign-in: a script without a solved captcha never reaches the password check, which is the server-side backstop the client lockout needed. GoTrue's own per-IP sign-in limit could NOT be measured (the captcha check answers first; solving captchas from a script is not possible). Evidence ~/.lh-shots/sec-a-q1121/probe-20261015003509.json. Proposed guard (not built): a nightly live check that a captcha-less password sign-in still answers captcha_failed, so turning captcha off goes red.

AppDelegate.swift application(_:open:options:) and (_:continue:restorationHandler:) both forward to ApplicationDelegateProxy (read, correct; no UIScene manifest in Info.plist, so these are the entry points); Info.plist registers the `helpr` scheme. ANDROID: there is no `android/` project in the repo, so no intent-filter or launchMode exists to check — Android deep links cannot work until one is generated (`npx cap add android`) and given a `helpr` intent-filter + App Links; not done here. - [ ] **Q1415 LOW Ban-review loose ends (lh-money-escrow + lh-authz-rls reviews of fbdfa47c7, 2026-10-06; Q1324 follow-up).** (a) A tip held by the review never gets the aged-out notice the payout-hold path sends (auto-tip-charge skips before that branch). (b) Release during a review answers 'Failed to update the job status, please try again' forever (trg_refuse_payout_pending_during_ban_review); give it neutral final copy. (c) Refunds to a poster under review (cancel_escrow, void-cancelled-payments) and money-in paths (create-boost-payment, claim-gift-card) are not refused; none pays the account under review, decide whether any should be. (d) An open review is not itself a payout block: if an admin releases the Q1324 system hold (or an admin hold pre-empted it), instant-payout, cash-out-credits and Stripe auto payouts read only payout_holds; make checkPayoutHold count an open review, or have admin_release_payout_hold refuse while one is open. (e) PRE-EXISTING, the larger one (lh-money-escrow re-review of f2644839e): a late cancellation pays a Helpr under review behind only the payout hold, and charges the poster. poster_cancel_job has no ban/review check and still charges the fee (cancellation_fee_percent(true, 1) = 50, measured live) and strikes the poster; void-cancelled-payments (single job and crew share), heldTipRepay and the chargeback pay-back claim through cancellation_fee_transfers or Stripe directly and check checkPayoutHold only. Fix: no fee and no strike when the Helpr (or a crew member) is under an open review or banned; a refusal trigger on cancellation_fee_transfers like refuse_payout_claim_while_held (or (d) above covers all hold-only paths at once). (f) A retained-ban user_bans row written during a review has banned_by = self, and the confirm (UPDATE user_bans ... WHERE banned_by = p_user_id) rewrites it to its own judgment, which can weaken a stricter identity judgment; the confirm should apply the strictest judgment recorded during the review (lh-authz-rls re-review of f2644839e). finding: lh-money-escrow@fbdfa47c7#4 **PARTLY FIXED 2026-10-12 (branch ready/todo-a-4): (b) and (d) done.** (d) admin_release_payout_hold refuses 'ban_review_open' while the Helpr's review is open (20261013000532; PGlite old RED / new GREEN 3x; class guard src/test/payoutHoldReleaseWaitsForBanReview.test.ts: every function that clears payout_holds refuses an open review or is the review's own decision), so every hold-only path (instant-payout, cash-out-credits, Stripe auto payouts, and the late-cancel fee paths of (e)) stays blocked until the review decides; the admin copy says so. (b) a release refused by trg_refuse_payout_pending_during_ban_review answers neutral, final copy (create-payment edge case). (a) DONE too: auto-tip-charge ages out a tip held by an open review past its 14-day window like a payout hold (recorded 'failed', the poster told once, in words that never name the review; edge cases in src/test/edge/banReviewFreezesMoney.test.ts). Review copy: the hold-release refusal says a lift releases the hold and a confirmed ban keeps it (lh-money-escrow review #11; a confirmed-banned account's system hold can still be released by an admin, as before). STILL OPEN: (c) owner decision (refunds/money-in for a poster under review), (e) the poster is still charged the fee and struck when the Helpr is under review or banned (their pay itself now waits on the hold), **(f) DONE 2026-10-12 (branch ready/todo-a-5):** ban_review_retained_floor (20261013001155) returns the strictest standing retained judgment applied during the review (auto-applied, uncleared, a live retained row, recorded or re-applied since the review opened, not a spent suspension; a no-end suspension outlasts a dated one, in any row order), and BOTH admin_confirm_ban_settlement and lift_ban_settlement_review take it as a floor (a lift clears the card/bank matches first). PGlite src/test/pglite/banReviewConfirmStrictest.pglite.mjs on prod's bodies (md5 checked): live 6 FAIL, fixed ALL PASS 3x; guard src/test/banReviewConfirmKeepsStrictest.test.ts (every review decision that writes the standing takes the floor first, and the floor's filters). lh-authz-rls re-review: clean, with three LOW follow-ups kept here: (f1) the 'reapplied since' branch reads retained_bans.reapplied_at, stamped for ANY account, so a pardoned match whose retained row survived the unban (retained_via 'deletion', 1 on prod) can count if someone else re-applies that row during the review; fix: stamp the match row itself in enforce_retained_ban's ON CONFLICT flip and drop that branch; (f2) PRE-EXISTING: a lift whose result is 'banned' writes 'banned' over the review's 'banned', so no settlement trigger fires and the account's jobs stay unsettled once the freeze ends (a permanently_banned floor settles as of now, not as of the review); fix: run confirm's explicit settlement when a lift ends banned; (f3) PRE-EXISTING: confirm's rung branch still uses COALESCE(original_expires_at, '-infinity'), so a dated rung can replace a no-end review suspension. Also: the admin console should show the status a lift returns (a lift can now keep an identity ban). finding: lh-authz-rls@df48cf219#1

GUARD: the "the Turnstile bot check (Q1589)" block in src/test/edge/signup-lead-reminders.test.ts (red on the old function: 5 of 7 fail) plus 3 cases in src/lib/signupLead.test.ts; the secret is classified in scripts/audit/expiry-inventory.json (no-expiry). The lh-authz-rls review-only pass (changes needed) found a single-use token race: TurnstileField.getToken now TAKES the token (two waiters never share one; red without it in src/components/auth/TurnstileField.test.tsx), the function logs every refusal reason, and it accepts only tokens minted for action "signup" (a login-form token answers 403). **WAITS ON THE OWNER:** the check is live only once the edge secret TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY holds the Turnstile widget's secret key (Cloudflare > Turnstile > Louisiana Helpr sign-in > Settings; the same one already in Supabase Auth's CAPTCHA setting); until then each capture is saved unchecked and the function logs the gap on every call. - [ ] **Q1727 MEDIUM The tax preview and the charge use different addresses (lh-money-escrow review of Q1578, 2026-10-12).** The preview prices the job ZIP (CheckoutStep.tsx ~166 -> calculate-tax ~120); Checkout taxes the poster's BILLING address (create-payment ~850 customer_update.address auto, no shipping address). Now that assembly/handyman are taxed (Q1578), a poster billed in another parish pays that parish's rate, and one billed out of state pays $0 with no alert (taxedZeroOnTaxableLouisianaLabor only fires for LA billing). Fix: give Stripe the job site as the tax address (Stripe prefers a shipping address), so preview == charge. GUARD when built: a create-payment edge case where billing != job ZIP and the session carries the job-site address, red first. finding: lh-money-escrow@6e679c20c#1 **TRIED 2026-10-12 (lane 1 money), NOT landed: the first design is unsafe.** Measured in Stripe test mode: a Customer's `shipping` beats its billing address for Checkout tax (billing Austin TX + shipping Delcambre LA taxed Louisiana). But writing the job site onto the shared Stripe Customer persists: Subscriptions and invoices also tax `customer.shipping` first, so a Pro/Plus subscription (txcd_10103001, taxable SaaS) and every renewal would be taxed at the last job site, and a ZIP outside Louisiana stamped as state LA may make Stripe refuse every automatic-tax checkout on that customer (customer_tax_location_invalid; unmeasured). Options for the next build: (a) Checkout without automatic_tax, with a precomputed "Sales tax" line from a tax.calculations call at the job site, committed with createFromCalculation on completion (what recurring visits already do); or (b) shipping_address_collection so the poster confirms the job address in Checkout. Either way, validate the ZIP is 700xx-714xx and send the same address shape to the preview (Q1657). **TRIED AGAIN 2026-10-12 (branch ready/todo-a-4), dropped before landing:** the build saved the job ZIP as customer.shipping before the session, i.e. the unsafe first design above; the lh-money-escrow review blocked it on the same grounds (create-pro-checkout taxes the same Customer with automatic_tax, so a Pro/Plus checkout and every renewal would carry the last job site; two overlapping checkouts can tax one job at the other's site). The next build is option (a) or (b) above.
  - Merged 2026-10-14 (same tax-address problem): Q1723 is ticked into this item and keeps its full text. From Q1723: measure one split-parish ZIP with a live Stripe Tax calculation (no charge) before choosing between options (a) and (b) above.

Fix: when the linked job is not on the list, say so (the job is no longer available) and fall back to the first bucket with anything; guard it in the same test.

- [~] **Q1675 LOW A job whose duplicate $2 setup fee was already refunded refunds the $2 again if it is later cancelled or disputed (lh-money-escrow review of Q1346, 2026-10-12; never happened on prod: 0 duplicate_onboarding_fee refund rows).** create-payment cancel_escrow (~2040), void-cancelled-payments (~1499-1519) and admin_refund_dispute (~2687) start from pi.amount_received, which does not go down after settleOnboardingFee's partial $2 refund, so each would return the $2 a second time out of platform money (cancel_escrow's alreadyRefundedCents check skips only when the earlier refund covers the whole amount). Fix: subtract refunds already ledgered on the PaymentIntent (payment_refunds by stripe_payment_intent_id) from the refundable base in all three. GUARD when built: an edge test per path with a prior duplicate_onboarding_fee refund row. finding: lh-money-escrow@c4e1df1bb#4 FIXED 2026-10-14 CT (sec-a): all three paths now take what earlier refunds already returned off the refundable amount through _shared/refundAmount.ts (alreadyRefundedCents). cancel_escrow and void-cancelled-payments read the charge's amount_refunded (Stripe is the authority; a void whose charge already shows everything refunded, e.g. its own refund with a lost ledger row past the 24 h key, now settles the status only instead of refunding the whole amount again); admin_refund_dispute lists the PaymentIntent's refunds and subtracts every live one that is not its own (metadata.source admin_refund_dispute), so the Q1209 retry still finds its own refund. The refund notices' kept amount no longer counts the earlier refund as kept. GUARD src/test/edge/create-payment.test.ts ("Q1675: a cancel after a duplicate $2 setup-fee refund ...", "Q1675: admin_refund_dispute after a duplicate $2 setup-fee refund ...", the Q1209 foreign-refund case now with a $50 foreign refund) and src/test/edge/void-cancelled-payments.test.ts (Q1675 describe, 3 cases): 5 of these FAIL on origin/main's source, and each of 7 @mutate lines was applied by hand and turned its test red. Pins in src/test/refundsKeepTheCardFee.test.ts updated (each amount still keeps the card fee). execute-dispute-split is not changed (it already refuses and pages on any refund that is not its own, so it never double-refunds). Money pass on the branch (lh-money-escrow@ba10093ae, changes needed, all fixed in the same branch): (1) a void whose charge was already refunded PAST what the cancel keeps no longer settles and pays the Helpr's fee; it pages money_at_risk and leaves the job (test "a charge already refunded past what the cancel keeps ..."); (2) cancel_escrow and admin_refund_dispute page money_at_risk in that case instead of logging a retry or blaming the processing fee; (6) the notices' kept amount is asserted (We kept $3.36 / $10); (7) a FAILED foreign refund is not subtracted (asserted) and the own-refund @mutate sits on the Q1209 reuse test. Not changed: (4) the code assumes Stripe's charge.amount_refunded counts pending refunds and drops a failed one (not verified against a real failed refund); (5) a changed amount under the same idempotency key can only meet a saved Stripe error inside 24 h, never a second refund. The reverse order (cancel first, duplicate-fee refund after) is Q1904. Still true (pre-existing, re-check of the money pass): a crew void pays its block fees (payBlockFees) BEFORE that page, so those still go out when the charge was refunded past the kept fees. done-when: test src/test/edge/create-payment.test.ts done-when: test src/test/edge/void-cancelled-payments.test.ts

- [ ] **Q1737 MEDIUM A missing /assets/ or /fonts/ file now 404s, but the 404 carries `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable` (found proving Q1720, 2026-10-12).** MEASURED with curl -sI on www (before 21:33Z, build c4b1b5254): /assets/missing-Q1663-x.js, /assets/notificationFeed-B4je1ZJb.js and /fonts/missing-x.woff2 all answer 404 text/plain with that header, because vercel.json's `/assets/(.*)` and `/fonts/(.*)` header rules stamp every response under those paths, found or not. A browser may keep a 404 with explicit freshness, so a chunk asked for in a deploy's propagation window (Q199) could stay a cached 404 under that URL. Not reproduced in a browser. Fix to weigh: limit the immutable header to files that exist (e.g. `missing` on the header rule, or a 404 page with no-store). GUARD when built: extend src/test/immutablePathsNeverServeTheSpa.test.ts, plus a live curl in a post-deploy check.
- [ ] **Q1739 LOW The saved-only feed and the saved count disagree when a saved job is not on the loaded pages or is not shown at all (lh-silent-failure review of Q1701, 2026-10-12, code read, not measured on prod).** savedJobIds (the saved count on the bookmark, Dashboard.tsx savedCount) holds every saved id, while the saved-only feed shows only saved jobs that are on a page already loaded AND pass every feed cull. A saved job on a later page, closed or filled, filtered out, applied to, dismissed, or from a blocked poster counts but never shows, so the count can say 3 while the feed says "Nothing saved yet". A load-more-until-found fix was tried and withdrawn in the Q1701 branch: it pages through the whole feed for jobs that will never show, and useLoadMoreSentinel only re-attaches on a dependency change, so the auto-paging did not start on its own. Needs: a server read of "saved and still shown to me" for both the count and the saved-only list (or the saved-only list read by id). GUARD when built: a test that the count and the saved-only list agree for a saved job on page 3 and for a saved closed job. finding: lh-silent-failure@28e0cfac5#2
- [ ] **Q1750 MEDIUM Gift cards stay on the LIVE Stripe key for test accounts (found building test mode for test accounts, 2026-10-12).** create-gift-card-checkout still builds its Stripe client from STRIPE_SECRET_KEY for everyone, so a test account buying a gift gets a live Checkout (the nightly prod-gift-card spec skips it as LIVE_PAY). It was not moved to test mode because a gift can go to ANY email address: a test buyer could hand test-mode credit to a real person. Needs a recipient rule first (a test buyer may gift only a test account, or the gift is refused), then route it through _shared/stripeMode.ts. Money change: it gets its own lh-money-escrow pass before landing. GUARD: src/test/stripeKeyChosenByMode.test.ts lists it in LIVE_ONLY; moving it to ROUTED is the done-when.
  - Merged 2026-10-14 (one problem: the LIVE_ONLY list in src/test/stripeKeyChosenByMode.test.ts; each entry must move to ROUTED in the same commit as its fix): Q1751 and Q1752 are ticked into this item and keep their full text.

  - Each of the three needs its own lh-money-escrow pass.
  - STATUS 2026-10-15 (sec-a, ready/sec-a-12): the GIFT-CARD part is built (lead rule: the BUYER decides). create-gift-card-checkout picks its key with _shared/stripeMode.ts: a real buyer pays LIVE whoever receives the gift (real money stays real, even to a test account); a test-account buyer pays on the TEST key and may gift ONLY another test account (a real account, or an email with no account, is refused 409 before Stripe; a failed recipient read refuses 503). stripe-webhook now routes a TEST-mode gift_card_purchase checkout but applies it only when donor AND recipient are both test accounts (testGiftIsTestToTest), so sandbox credit can never be claimed by a real sign-up. create-gift-card-checkout moved LIVE_ONLY -> ROUTED in src/test/stripeKeyChosenByMode.test.ts; behaviour pinned by src/test/edge/giftCardStripeMode.test.ts (10 of 12 red on the old source, every @mutate red). STILL OPEN here: Stripe Identity (still matters) and Pro (can wait: memberships are being switched off by the other session). The consequence of the buyer rule (a live gift spent by a test account on a test job) is Q1905. lh-money-escrow review (approve): its finding 1 fixed here (claim-gift-card refuses a TEST-key gift to anyone not a test account, fail closed) and finding 4 fixed (the mode is in the idempotency key); findings 2 and 3 are Q1906 and Q1907.
- [ ] **Q1754 MEDIUM The e2e sweeper still settles hired+funded leftovers forward only when the repo variable E2E_STRIPE_MODE is "test", which means "all of prod runs the test key" (found building test mode for test accounts, 2026-10-12; `gh variable list` reads E2E_STRIPE_MODE=live, set 2026-09-28T00:41Z).** scripts/e2e/prod-lifecycle-sweeper.mjs (STRIPE_TEST_MODE, CAN_SETTLE_FORWARD) and the five workflows that pass the variable assume ONE key for all of prod. With test mode for test accounts, a cs_test_ row on the shared test accounts is settled by create-payment `release` on the TEST key whatever the live key is, so the variable no longer says what the sweeper needs to know; until this changes every sweep defers those rows (Q421's settled-escrow sweep log cannot appear). Decide: drop the variable for rows whose Checkout Session is cs_test_ (keeping settleRefusalReason's cs_test_ rule), or redefine it; update stripe-sandbox-on.sh/-off.sh and src/test/sweeperFailsClosed.test.ts with it. - [ ] **Q1755 MEDIUM A test account can apply to (and be hired on) a REAL job, which puts a test account on real money (found by the lh-authz-rls and lh-money-escrow reviews of the test-mode branch, 2026-10-12; the 2026-10-09 incident was exactly this: the shared test Helpr applied to the owner's real job).** enforce_application_job_state rule C6 only stops a REAL user applying to a seed job (`v_job.is_seed AND seed_hidden_for(helper)`); nothing stops the reverse, nor a direct offer or accept between a real poster and a test account. With test mode for test accounts the money side is safe (a payout of live escrow to a test Helpr refuses as mixed_parties, and the poster's refund still runs in live mode), but such a job can never be paid out and needs a refund. Fix at the source: refuse a test account applying to, being offered or accepting a non-seed job (and a real account on a seed job, as today), in the trigger and the offer/accept RPCs. Data-model change: migration, PGlite 3x, lh-authz-rls review. GUARD when built: a PGlite test that a test-account application to a real job is refused, red on today's trigger. finding: lh-authz-rls@8e3b0e622#1 Note (lh-authz-rls review of 000ca9c1e, info #2, measured 2026-10-12: 57 test_accounts rows, all is_seed; besides 55 fixture emails they include the seed admin d3128db4 and apple-reviewer f2098932): the App Store review account pays on the TEST key, so on any REAL job it gets create-payment's 409 "This job pairs a test account with a real account"; App Review must be pointed at seed jobs.
  - Merged 2026-10-14 (same cause: a test account reaching real jobs): Q1758 is ticked into this item and keeps its full text. From Q1758: a mixed test/real cancelled job refunds the whole fee and leaves ledger rows pending; unreachable once this item lands.
- [ ] **Q1756 LOW Re-minting a test account's checkout leaves its old LIVE Checkout Session open until it expires (found by the lh-money-escrow review of the test-mode branch, 2026-10-12).** create-payment escrow skips reading a cs_live_ session on a job that now runs in test mode (the test key cannot read it) and mints a cs_test_ one, but does not expire the live one, which Stripe keeps payable for up to 24 hours. Measured 2026-10-12 by the reviewer: 0 of the 68 abandoned and 2 unpaid cs_live_ sessions on test accounts' jobs were ever paid; after deploy no new live session is minted for a test account. Expiring it needs the LIVE key on a test account's request (the one live call this design avoids); decide whether that is worth it. finding: lh-money-escrow@7389f2a30#6
- [~] **Q1759 LOW charge-recurring-visits settles money ALREADY taken (a paid visit's refund, an open visit Checkout's expiry) as NEW money, so a mixed series is skipped instead of refunded (found by the lh-money-escrow review of the test-mode branch, 2026-10-12; 0 recurring_visit_payments rows on prod, measured by the review).** The stale/series-over loop takes its client with kind "new" from the parent's parties plus the visit's payer and Helpr (charge-recurring-visits/index.ts ~734-742), and uses it both to expire an open visit Checkout and to refund a PAID visit. That breaks the rule in _shared/stripeMode.ts (money already taken runs where it sits; a refund needs only the payer): a real payer's series with a test Helpr on a visit (the Q1755 entry path) never auto-refunds its live money (a defect every run) and never expires an open live Checkout. The mode also comes from the parties, not the visit row's own cs_live_/cs_test_ session, and create-payment's recurring_visit mode counts only the parent's parties while the cron also counts the visit's Helpr, so a visit Checkout can be minted in a mode the cron then refuses. Fix: decide expire and refund with kind "refund" from the visit row's own session prefix. Money change: lh-money-escrow pass. GUARD when built: an edge test of a mixed series whose paid visit is refunded on the payer's key. **STRANDED WIP 2026-10-14 (stranded2-1013 lane):** an unreviewed build covering Q1759, Q1760, Q1761 and the fully gift-funded job's late-cancellation fee (its commit says "WIP for review", lane A2, 2026-10-12) sits on local claim/Q1781 = origin wip/stranded2-1013-q1781-wip @ 80d2480f2. Start from it and give it its lh-money-escrow pass; it was not landed because the owner ordered no new to-do work during the 1.0.4 release (lead, 2026-10-13). finding: lh-money-escrow@000ca9c1e#2 **BUILT 2026-10-15 (sec-a, ready/sec-a-10; rebuilt from the stranded WIP 80d2480f2):** the stale/series-over loop in charge-recurring-visits decides an open visit Checkout's expiry and a PAID visit's refund with visitMoneyClient: the visit row's own Checkout Session prefix (cs_live_/cs_test_) where the money sits, else its payer alone (a refund needs only the payer); a cs_test_ row of a real payer stays on the live key, as jobMoneyMode decides for a job. The visit's Helpr and the series' parties no longer count, so a real payer's series with a test Helpr on a visit is refunded live instead of skipped as a mix, and a visit Checkout that create-payment minted from the parent's parties is no longer refused by the cron. After the lh-money-escrow review: a cs_live_ row needs no test-account read (a failed read never holds back a live refund), and a failed read on a row with no prefix fails closed (a defect, no refund). The payer alone decides a cs_test_ row (jobMoneyMode would count every job party): only sandbox money can land on the wrong key, where it is skipped, never moved. GUARD src/test/edge/charge-recurring-visits.test.ts (3 Q1759 cases, 2 @mutate each shown red; 2 of the 3 FAIL on main's source, all pass after). Left for the review round: lh-money-escrow review on the landed sha. done-when: test src/test/edge/charge-recurring-visits.test.ts

- [~] **Q1761 LOW A Stripe TEST-mode webhook event that names nobody on its own object is applied, and its handler then follows references with the test key (found by the lh-authz-rls and lh-money-escrow reviews of the test-mode branch, 2026-10-12).** testEventScope (_shared/stripeMode.ts) reads only the event object's metadata, client_reference_id, emails and Connect account owner; an event naming nobody passes, on the stated assumption that only rows a test payment wrote hold its ids. Disputes and refunds carry no metadata, and chargeRefunded.ts retrieves the PaymentIntent on the test key and merges ITS metadata into the refund notice, which picks the recipient from job_id / customer_id / payer_id (refundNotice.ts ~344-358): a sandbox refund of an old pre-live charge whose PaymentIntent names a real user would notify (in-app and email) a real person about sandbox money. Fix: on a test-mode event, refuse any refund-notice (and any notice) recipient who is not a test account, and add a nightly check that no non-seed row holds a test-mode Stripe id. GUARD when built: a stripe-webhook edge test of a test-mode charge.refunded whose PaymentIntent metadata names a real user: no notice sent. **STRANDED WIP 2026-10-14 (stranded2-1013 lane):** an unreviewed build covering Q1759, Q1760, Q1761 and the fully gift-funded job's late-cancellation fee (its commit says "WIP for review", lane A2, 2026-10-12) sits on local claim/Q1781 = origin wip/stranded2-1013-q1781-wip @ 80d2480f2. Start from it and give it its lh-money-escrow pass; it was not landed because the owner ordered no new to-do work during the 1.0.4 release (lead, 2026-10-13). finding: lh-authz-rls@000ca9c1e#1 finding: lh-money-escrow@000ca9c1e#4 **BUILT 2026-10-15 (sec-a, ready/sec-a-10; rebuilt from the stranded WIP 80d2480f2):** chargeRefunded passes testMode (event.livemode === false) to sendRefundNotice, which, once it has picked the recipient (from the job, the PaymentIntent's metadata or the payer), tells only a TEST account about sandbox money: a real person comes back 'dropped' (neither the in-app notice nor the email is written), and so does a failed test-account read (losing a sandbox notice beats telling a real person). GUARD src/test/edge/refundNotice.test.ts (3 Q1761 cases: real person -> nothing told + finished claim; failed read -> nothing told + finished claim; test account still told; 4 @mutate each shown red; the first two FAIL on main's source). The nightly check that no non-seed row holds a test-mode Stripe id is split out as its own item (Q1909). Left for the review round: lh-authz-rls + lh-money-escrow review on the landed sha. done-when: test src/test/edge/refundNotice.test.ts

- [ ] **Q1802 LOW A recurring-series date a Helpr held BEFORE their saved birthday became under 18 is still booked by the cron (lh-authz-rls review of 30e7034c4, Q1586(c); 0 under-18 birthdays on prod 2026-10-12, so unreachable today).** Q1586(c) refuses every NEW series_visit_holds row for an under-18 member, but a hold that already exists (claimed while the birthday was empty or adult, then an admin corrects it) is still turned into a visit by charge-recurring-visits: it charges the poster and inserts the visit with helper_id set (a service insert, not judged), then the applications upsert is refused by trg_applications_refuse_under_18 and the cron pages ops 'Recurring visit created without its application row'. That is actual knowledge that a child under 13 is using the app (COPPA). Options for the owner: keep the account greyed out like any minor; or close and delete an under-13 account on save; or refuse to keep the personal details until a parent is involved. Measured: 0 under-18 birthdays on prod 2026-10-12 (the reviewer's read). finding: lh-authz-rls@44ca75e95#5 **NOTE 2026-10-14 (owner, Q1823):** no screen asks a birthday now: PhotoPrompt is gone and Complete Profile's birthday wheel was replaced by the 18+ box (owner, ~3 PM CT). - [ ] **Q1806 LOW A fully gift-funded job's late-cancellation fee is never paid to the Helpr (found doing Q1490, 2026-10-12; code read, not seen on prod: 0 redeemed gift cards on a job).** void-cancelled-payments prices the fee (computeCancellationFee, or the crew shares) BEFORE it resolves the PaymentIntent, but a fully gift-funded job has none and takes the 'no payment found' branch: it settles payment_status 'cancelled' with the whole gift restored and pays no fee, and Parts D/E only retry jobs whose payment_status is 'refunded' with the fee 'charged'. So a Helpr (or crew) cancelled on late is owed a fee nobody pays. Fix to weigh: pay the fee from the gift (restore the gift less the fee, as Q1490 does for block fees, and pay it from the platform balance with a ledger row), with the same no-fee rules as a card-paid job. GUARD when built: a void-cancelled-payments edge case for a gift-funded single job and crew with a late cancel. The same class, from the lh-money-escrow review of Q1490 (#9): a crew paid partly by gift and partly by card whose card capture is smaller than its block fees still restores the whole gift (settleCancelledJob on the card branches), and Part F pays the rest of the fee from the platform balance. STATUS 2026-10-15 (sec-a-17): the fully gift-funded part is FIXED in void-cancelled-payments: the no-payment branch reads what the gift paid (a dry run; after a restore only what it kept), puts the fee on its ledger and sends it FIRST from the platform balance the gift paid into (no charge to link), restores the gift LESS the fee (and block fees), and settles the job cancelled + cancellation_fee_status charged, so Parts D/E (now reading 'cancelled' gift jobs too, paid with no PaymentIntent) retry a fee that could not go yet. No gift and no card: no fee is paid from the platform's own money. An unreadable gift, a failed ledger claim or a gift smaller than the fees settles nothing (fails closed). Measured on prod 2026-10-15: 22 gift_cards rows, 0 with a job_id, so nobody is owed one today. GUARD: src/test/edge/void-cancelled-payments-gift-fee.test.ts (single, crew, Parts D/E, fail-closed cases), plus a PGlite run of the real restore_gift_card_for_job (old keeps 0c of a 15000c fee, new keeps it; 3 runs). Review (lh-money-escrow@2f1c33aa4, changes-needed): FIXED here #1 (a gift whose donation was refunded or charged back funds no fee: settled without it and a person told), #5 (a cancelled job WITH a PaymentIntent, a voided hold, is never retried as a gift job), #6 (a gift an earlier run already gave back without the fee settles without it instead of sitting in escrow), #7 (tests: revoked gift, re-run after fee paid, fee plus block fee); re-review approve (its nit 1 fixed: no spent gift row back fails closed; nit 2, a donation reversed after the fee went out leaves the job uncharged, is named in the alert); 20 tests, 18 @mutate lines each shown red, 17 of 20 red on the old function; #2, #3, #4, #8 filed as their own items. STILL OPEN: the mixed case below (a crew paid partly by gift and partly by card whose capture is smaller than its fees still restores the whole gift). finding: lh-money-escrow@e45c44f44#9 **STATUS 2026-10-15 (sec-a), NOT BUILT TONIGHT, measured:** prod gift_cards = 22 rows, 0 with a job_id, 0 redeemed (execute_sql 07:07Z), so no Helpr is owed this fee today. The fix is not one branch: a fully gift-funded single job needs the fee priced and withheld from the gift in the no-payment branch (settleCancelledJob/restorePifGift take only crew block-fee cents now), paid from the platform balance with no source charge (payHelperCancellationFee and payCrewCancellationFees both take a PaymentIntent), and Parts D and E both skip a job with no stripe_payment_intent_id as a defect, so a fee that cannot go on the first run would never be retried. That is three money paths plus an lh-money-escrow review, more than fits before the proof round; next sec-a builds it with the GUARD named above.

NOT measured: whether the map drew anything for that visitor, or which MapKit feature wants WebAssembly. Next: reproduce on /home with the map in WebKit and Chromium, see whether mk-csr.js degrades or breaks, then either allow 'wasm-unsafe-eval' (CSP hash test, and lh-appsec signs off) or drop it as known noise in csp-report's isNoise with a test.
- [ ] **Q1822 LOW In Playwright WebKit the admin Jump To palette is see-through over a second open dialog (found 2026-10-14 proving Q1786, measured).** prod 904f3f3a1, seed-admin at /admin, 375, with the Terms re-agree alertdialog open underneath (the seed admin had not re-agreed): in WebKit the page behind is not blurred, and the re-agree card's text shows through the palette's search field and list (~/.lh-shots/proof3-1014/out/q1786-palette-375-wk2.png, recorded as a defect). Chromium shows both blurred. Both engines compute the same styles: overlay blur(24px) saturate(1.5), dialogs blur(40px) over rgba(240,242,244,.95). WebKit reports CSS.supports("-webkit-backdrop-filter") false and the unprefixed one true. NOT measured: whether a real iPhone (WKWebView) shows the same. It may be the stacked-backdrop-filter paint class behind the Linux WebKit Filters stall (wip/proof2-1014-blur), or only a Playwright-WebKit screenshot limit. Next: look on the simulator with two dialogs open.
- [ ] **Q1826 LOW OWNER DECISION: should deleting an account clear its 18+ box record? (lh-authz-rls review of the noprompt branch, 2026-10-14).** purge_user_data and wipe_pre_verification_account set date_of_birth to NULL but do not touch the new profiles.age_attested_at (a bare timestamp of when the member ticked "I confirm I am 18 years of age or older"). It is not personal data on an anonymised row, and it may be worth keeping as a legal record of the attestation. Options: keep it (say so beside both functions), or clear it (needs the pre-verification-wipe flag, which pin_age_attested_at already honours). finding: lh-authz-rls@9d35e9f18#5
- [ ] **Q1827 LOW A crew refund re-run can owe a few cents more than the refund it already made, and page ops for them (lh-money-escrow review of 79e9a6bb5, #3; code read, not measured).** Since 2026-10-14 process-scheduled-payouts returns a crew's fee share only above Stripe's card fee (crewFeeShareReturnCents), and that fee is the charge's real balance-transaction fee when it can be read, else the card-rate estimate. A run that used the estimate and a later run that reads a lower real fee compute different cents, so cardOwedCents rises above the refund already ledgered and the shortRefund branch pages for the difference; the comment "a re-run owes the same cents" is no longer strictly true. Also unverified (lh-silent-failure review of 79e9a6bb5, #7): if one crew charge could get BOTH a crew dispute refund and an unfilled-spot refund, each caps the fee share on its own and the two together could return part of the card fee. Fix: stamp the fee the first run used (refund metadata) and reuse it; check whether both refunds can hit one charge. GUARD when built: a process-scheduled-payouts-crew case of a re-run whose fee read changed, and one of a dispute plus an unfilled refund on one charge. finding: lh-money-escrow@79e9a6bb5#3
- [ ] **Q1828 LOW Every taxed recurring-visit refund that keeps the card fee is a PARTIAL refund at Stripe, so its sales-tax reversal is paged to ops by hand (lh-money-escrow review of 79e9a6bb5, #6; lh-silent-failure review of 79e9a6bb5, #5; code read).** stripe-webhook/handlers/_visitTaxReversal.ts reverses a visit's Stripe Tax transaction automatically only on a FULL refund; a partial one raises "Taxed recurring visit partly refunded: reverse its sales tax by hand". The never-absorb rule (owner, 2026-10-14) makes every fee-keeping visit refund partial, now including a series an admin ended (before, those were full and reversed automatically). Money is right; the tax report needs a person each time. Fix: reverse the refunded share automatically (Stripe Tax transactions.createReversal mode partial with the refunded amount), keyed on the refund id. Not checked by either reviewer: how Stripe Tax handles a partial refund of a Checkout automatic_tax JOB charge (admin full refunds are now partial too). GUARD when built: a stripe-webhook case of a fee-withheld visit refund reversing its tax share with no page. finding: lh-money-escrow@79e9a6bb5#6
- [ ] **Q1829 LOW A duplicate-funding refund whose gift restore fails is never retried: the redelivery skips the restore (lh-money-escrow re-review of dfae918ae, 2026-10-14).** checkoutSessionCompleted's duplicate-funding branch throws when the gift restore UPDATE errors ("The refund is keyed, so the retry this throw causes refunds nothing twice and tries the restore again"), but on Stripe's redelivery the gift is already 'redeemed' on this job, the consume step logs "Reserved gift card already consumed for this job, skipping" and leaves giftConsumedHere false, so the restore never runs and the gift stays spent on a job it did not fund (code read, not seen on prod). The Q1819 ledger write was moved after the restore for this reason (ready/sec-a-1). Fix: on the duplicate branch, restore when the gift is redeemed on this job by THIS checkout (session metadata gift_card_id), not only when this delivery consumed it. GUARD when built: a stripe-webhook edge case where the restore errors, then a redelivery asserts the gift is back to 'sent'. finding: lh-money-escrow@dfae918ae#1
- [ ] **Q1913 LOW A crew with no PaymentIntent and no gift still pays its crew block fees from the platform's own money (lh-money-escrow review of Q1806, 2026-10-15; code read, 0 gift_cards with a job_id on prod).** void-cancelled-payments' no-payment branch calls payCrewBlockFees(..., null) whenever a block fee is owed, and Part F pays any left owed on the settled job, without checking that a gift paid for the job (Q1490 behaviour; Q1806 now checks this for the late-cancellation fee only). Reachable by a seed crew in escrow with no PaymentIntent, or a gift an earlier run gave back whole. Fix: read giftFeeFunding when block fees are owed too; with no money behind the job, void the rows (or leave them for a person) and alert instead of paying. GUARD when built: a void-cancelled-payments edge case for an unfunded no-PaymentIntent crew with an owed block fee. finding: lh-money-escrow@2f1c33aa4#2
- [ ] **Q1914 LOW A gift restored less a fee can keep up to about applied/10000 cents MORE than the fee (lh-money-escrow review of Q1806, 2026-10-15; measured in PGlite on the real restore_gift_card_for_job: a $330 gift less a $150 fee keeps $150.02).** restore_gift_card_for_job only takes a whole-basis-point share, so restorePifGift picks the largest bps that does not overshoot and the poster loses the rounding (their notice says $150 paid the fee and $179.98 is back). Since Q1806 every late-cancelled gift job goes through it, not only crews with block fees. Fix: a new migration adding an exact p_restore_cents (or p_withhold_cents) to restore_gift_card_for_job (replay-safe, grants restated, PGRST202 fallback), then assert restoredCents() === applied - fee exactly in void-cancelled-payments-gift-fee.test.ts. finding: lh-money-escrow@2f1c33aa4#3
- [ ] **Q1915 LOW OWNER A deleted crew member's late-cancellation share is kept from the poster (gift or card) and paid to nobody (lh-money-escrow review of Q1806, 2026-10-15; code read).** crewCancellationFee totals every share, including one whose member's account was deleted (helper_id NULL); payCrewCancellationFees only alerts on that share, and Part D re-alerts it every run with no oncePerDayKey. The block-fee path voids a deleted member's fee instead (Q1490 review #7). Owner call for BOTH paths: give the share back to the poster, or keep it; then build it the same on the card and gift paths, with a oncePerDayKey on the alert. GUARD when built: a crew edge case with a NULL-helper share. finding: lh-money-escrow@2f1c33aa4#4
- [ ] **Q1916 LOW A gift-funded job's cancellation fee shows as 'charged' to the poster, and a Helpr's still-pending fee says 'You received' (lh-money-escrow review of Q1806, 2026-10-15; code read, not seen on a screen).** PostedJobCard shows 'Fee $X · charged' in the destructive colour on a gift-funded job, where no card was charged (the gift paid it); CancellationFeePill tells the Helpr 'You received' while the fee row is still pending or failed (a payout hold, no payout account), true before Q1806 and now on gift jobs too. - [~] **Q1849 LOW An Apple charge that never asks the purchase gate is granted silently for a member the memberships switch is off for (lh-money-escrow review of 7f15e406d, should-fix 1; code read + measured).** subscription_purchase_eligibility (20261015073024) stops 7119's in-app purchase before the StoreKit sheet, but Apple auto-renewal, resubscribing from iOS Settings, promoted IAPs / offer codes and pre-approved StoreKit transactions reach verify-apple-iap and apple-app-store-notifications without it, and those must keep granting (never refuse after Apple charged). Fix: without refusing anything, verify-apple-iap and ASSN SUBSCRIBED/DID_RENEW raise a money_at_risk alert when they grant to a user feature_enabled_for_user('memberships') is false for, so a member charged for nothing is found and refunded. Guards src/test/edge/verify-apple-iap.test.ts and src/test/edge/apple-app-store-notifications.test.ts (Q1849 blocks, red on main's code). done-when: test src/test/edge/verify-apple-iap.test.ts

- [ ] **Q1852 MEDIUM declutter_seller_hidden(uuid) answers any signed-in member, for any uuid with a live or reserved Declutter listing, whether that seller is banned, has 3 Declutter strikes, is under 18 or is flagged business-like (lh-authz-rls review 2026-10-16; code read of 20261014210518 + declutter_seller_out).** It is the declutter_items read policy's helper, so authenticated holds EXECUTE, and a direct call gives a per-uuid yes/no the policy itself does not (a hidden seller looks like one with no listings); member_under_18 is deliberately revoked from authenticated (20261013010621). Found when db-deploy run 38836083553's Q1284 check listed it; it is under "unscoped" in scripts/ci/definer-exec-allowlist.json with this gap stated. Exposure today: Declutter is testers-only. Fix: keep the hidden state on declutter_items itself (a server-maintained column set by the ban / strike / age / business-flag paths), make the read policy test that column, then REVOKE EXECUTE on declutter_seller_hidden FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated and drop it from the allowlist. GUARD when built: the policy no longer calls a client-executable helper (pg_policies read in scripts/check-live-privileges.mjs's definer inventory) and the allowlist entry is gone (two-way). finding: lh-authz-rls@61c02d214#2
- [ ] **Q1888 LOW A member the seller blocked still reads that seller's live Declutter listings, and now their in_moving_sale flag (lh-authz-rls re-review 2026-10-16, measured in PGlite on wip/declutter-moving-out).** The declutter_items read policy (20261014210518 section 8) checks the switch and declutter_seller_hidden but never user_blocks, so a blocked buyer still browses the blocker's items and, since moving-out mode, learns the blocker has a moving sale (never the date or month: declutter_seller_moving_sale returns nothing for a blocked pair). Fix: add NOT public.are_users_blocked((SELECT auth.uid()), seller_id) to the policy's non-seller branch, with a PGlite proof that the seller still reads their own items and a blocked pair reads none. done-when: a guard reads the newest declutter_items read policy and finds the block check. finding: lh-authz-rls@217bbe0ac#2

Reachable only if a member's profile row does not exist (it is created at sign-up and user_id is unique), so LOW. Fix once Apple has decided on 1.0.4 (the freeze forbids narrowing a client privilege until then): add the five to the WITH CHECK; GUARD when built: extend src/test/billingColumnsArePinned.test.ts to read the newest INSERT policy.
- [~] **Q1853 HIGH The iPhone app's Apple membership purchase hands StoreKit no product id, and never binds the buyer (found by sec-c reading cordova-plugin-purchase 13.18.0 while building Q1796, 2026-10-15).** src/lib/iap.ts purchaseTier called `store.order(productId)` with a string; v13's store.order(offer) reads offer.platform and offer.productId (node_modules/cordova-plugin-purchase/www/store.js order, and the Apple adapter's `bridge.purchase(offer.productId, ...)`), and the Apple adapter hands `offer.productId` (undefined) to its StoreKit 1 bridge, which refuses an unregistered id without ever answering, so by the source the purchase promise never settles: nothing is charged and the Upgrade button spins forever (lh-silent-failure, checking 094308d53, traced the hang). Nothing set store.applicationUsername, so Apple's appAccountToken was never set and verify-apple-iap took its legacy grant-and-flag branch. Measured by reading the plugin source only: NOT seen failing on a device, and whether the plugin is inside the 7119 binary was not checked. Affects the Pro purchase Apple's reviewer tries (Q1794). **FIXED 2026-10-15 (sec-c, ready/sec-c-5), client only, needs a new build:** purchaseTier orders `store.get(id, Platform.APPLE_APPSTORE).getOffer()` after setting applicationUsername to the signed-in user id (SK1 + the default 'legacy' obfuscator passes it unchanged, store.js resolveBridgeUsername), refuses without a user or a loaded product (nothing charged). From that check (SAFE WITH FIXES): obfuscator pinned to 'disabled' so a later StoreKit 2 bridge cannot hash the id into a token verify-apple-iap 403s; a missing product is an IapBlockedError whose copy is shown; the buyer closing Apple's sheet is not an error toast. Guard src/lib/iap.order.test.ts (a fake v13 store that buys only an offer: red on the old iap.ts, 7 cases green after; all four @mutate killed). Left: a sandbox purchase on a device build. done-when: test src/lib/iap.order.test.ts
- [ ] **Q1854 HIGH Prod has no App Store Server API key, so no Apple purchase can be verified (found by sec-c 2026-10-15).** `supabase secrets list --project-ref fncmgoasalhdgfwzhsqa` (names only, 2026-10-15 ~01:20Z) lists 30 secrets and none of APPLE_IAP_ISSUER_ID, APPLE_IAP_KEY_ID, APPLE_IAP_PRIVATE_KEY, APPLE_IAP_BUNDLE_ID (nor APPLE_IAP_ENVIRONMENT). _shared/appleAppStore.ts readAppleConfig() throws "Apple IAP not configured" without the four, so verify-apple-iap answers 500 to every Apple membership purchase (Apple's reviewer's included, Q1794) and apple-app-store-notifications 500s every notice. OWNER: create an In-App Purchase key in App Store Connect (Users and Access > Integrations > In-App Purchase), then the four secrets are set (bundle id com.Helpr, the appId in capacitor.config.ts). Server-only, no rebuild, allowed during the freeze. GUARD when built: a live check that fails when any of the four names is missing from the project's secrets (names only), run by a nightly workflow.

Prod 2026-10-15: 0 rows with an Apple anchor. Fix: the daily subscription-reconciliation reclaims rows with apple_original_transaction_id, subscription_source not 'apple' and nothing held by Stripe, from Apple's latest state (needs Q1860's status read); until then the double-billing alert should tell ops to ask the member to Restore. GUARD when built: an edge test in src/test/edge/subscription-reconciliation-apple.test.ts. finding: lh-money-escrow@18f0d5fb3#3
- [~] **Q1859 LOW The double-billing alert can be replayed and overstates "paying twice" (lh-money-escrow re-review, 2026-10-15).** apple-app-store-notifications is unauthenticated, so anyone holding a real transaction id can POST it repeatedly and fire one #ops-alerts message per POST (no dedupe); and the alert says "paying twice" also when Stripe holds the row only through a one-time pass or a tier with no expiry. Fix: dedupe on (user_id, transactionId) per day and word the alert by which arm matched. GUARD when built: an edge test that two identical notices raise one alert. finding: lh-money-escrow@18f0d5fb3#6 **FIXED 2026-10-17 (sec-a, ready/sec-a-1017-1):** the double-billing alert now passes oncePerDayKey apple-double-billing:<user>:<originalTransactionId> (postSlackOpsAlert posts the first per UTC day, later ones go to the digest), and says "paying twice" only when a live Stripe subscription id is on the row; a Stripe tier with no subscription behind it is worded as a one-time pass or a tier with no end date, and tells ops the member can Restore once it ends. Guard src/test/edge/apple-app-store-notifications.test.ts (Q1859 block, red on main's code). done-when: test src/test/edge/apple-app-store-notifications.test.ts
- [~] **Q1860 HIGH An old or retried Apple notice can revoke a paid-up Apple member (lh-money-escrow re-review, pre-existing, 2026-10-15).** apple-app-store-notifications grades entitlement from the transaction id in the (unauthenticated) notice body. Apple retries failed notices for up to 3 days, so a retry arriving after a newer renewal (or a forged POST naming an older transaction of the same subscription) carries a past expiresDate and clears the tier of a member who has paid. Prod 2026-10-15: 0 Apple members, so nobody hit yet; blocked anyway until Q1854. Fix: re-read the subscription's latest state by originalTransactionId (App Store Server API GET /inApps/v1/subscriptions/{originalTransactionId}) and grade from that, never from the posted transaction. GUARD when built: an edge test where a stale transaction for a subscription whose latest status is active writes nothing. finding: lh-money-escrow@18f0d5fb3#8 **FIXED 2026-10-17 (sec-a, ready/sec-a-1017-1):** an auto-renewable notice is now graded from the subscription's LATEST transaction, read from Apple by originalTransactionId (_shared/appleAppStore.ts fetchLatestSubscriptionTransaction, GET /inApps/v1/subscriptions/{id} in the environment that served the posted transaction); a status lookup failure, or an answer that does not name this subscription, writes nothing and answers 500 so Apple retries. A one-time pass is still graded from its own transaction. Re-applying what Apple says now is a write of the latest expiry, never a clearing. Prod measured 2026-10-17 before the fix: the deployed function (v867) called only /inApps/v1/transactions, i.e. graded the posted transaction; 0 profiles with an Apple anchor. Still needs Q1854's App Store key to run at all. Guard src/test/edge/apple-app-store-notifications.test.ts (Q1860 block, 4 cases red on main's code). **ALSO, same root cause, still open (lh-money-escrow review of 54e7b92b7, #3):** verify-apple-iap (the signed-in Restore / purchase path) still grades the transaction id the client posts, so restoring an OLDER transaction of a renewed subscription writes a past expiry; route meta.kind === 'auto' through fetchLatestSubscriptionTransaction there too, with an edge case in src/test/edge/verify-apple-iap.test.ts. done-when: test src/test/edge/apple-app-store-notifications.test.ts
