- [ ] **Q1880 LOW void-cancelled-payments Part C query includes ACH-pending jobs in its page scope (ME-046 non-blocking observation, lh-money-escrow R4 review of PR #3197, 2026-10-16).** The abandonment sweep's Part C iterates over jobs with `status='open'` + `payment_status='unpaid'` for pagination/count purposes. After ME-046, an ACH checkout-completed job keeps `payment_status='unpaid'` and `stripe_session_id` set; the write guard (`stripe_session_id IS NULL`) prevents it from being abandoned, but it still appears in the page-scope query. Under high load with many ACH-pending jobs, this could inflate the page counts or slow the sweep. Verify whether Part C's WHERE clause filters on `stripe_session_id IS NULL` the same way the write does; if not, add the filter to the scope query. GUARD when built: a test that an ACH-pending job (`payment_status='unpaid'`, `stripe_session_id` set) is excluded from the sweep's iteration scope. finding: lh-money-escrow@41c7848c9#1
- [x] **Q1880 LOW void-cancelled-payments Part C query includes ACH-pending jobs in its page scope (ME-046 non-blocking observation, lh-money-escrow R4 review of PR #3197, 2026-10-16).** The abandonment sweep's Part C iterates over jobs with `status='open'` + `payment_status='unpaid'` for pagination/count purposes. After ME-046, an ACH checkout-completed job keeps `payment_status='unpaid'` and `stripe_session_id` set; the write guard (`stripe_session_id IS NULL`) prevents it from being abandoned, but it still appears in the page-scope query. Under high load with many ACH-pending jobs, this could inflate the page counts or slow the sweep. Verify whether Part C's WHERE clause filters on `stripe_session_id IS NULL` the same way the write does; if not, add the filter to the scope query. GUARD when built: a test that an ACH-pending job (`payment_status='unpaid'`, `stripe_session_id` set) is excluded from the sweep's iteration scope. finding: lh-money-escrow@41c7848c9#1 **CLOSED BY DESIGN 2026-10-17 (sec-a, ready/sec-a-1017-8), code read + measured:** the sweep is Part B (open + unpaid, older than 1 h), not Part C, and its write guard is not `stripe_session_id IS NULL`: Part B abandons a job with a session only when Stripe says that session EXPIRED unpaid (Q771), so an ACH-pending job (session complete, payment_status unpaid) is read but never abandoned; the cost is one sessions.retrieve per such job per run. Narrowing the read on stripe_payment_intent_id IS NULL is NOT safe: create-payment's stampSession re-mints a failed/abandoned job to 'unpaid' and keeps the stale PaymentIntent, so its expired re-mint would never be abandoned. Prod 2026-10-17: 2 open + unpaid jobs, 0 with a session, 0 with a PaymentIntent. GUARD: src/test/edge/void-cancelled-payments.test.ts "Q1880: Part B never abandons an ACH-pending job" (2 @mutate killed: abandon on unpaid alone, and no abandon of an expired session).
- [ ] **Q1879 LOW `abandoned` is a dead end for ACH: paymentIntentSucceeded cannot promote it to `escrow` (ME-046 non-blocking observation, lh-money-escrow R4 review of PR #3197, 2026-10-16).** The `paymentIntentSucceeded.ts` CAS filter is `payment_status IN (null, unpaid, failed)`. If void-cancelled-payments marks a job `abandoned` (ACH checkout created, then 1 h passes with `stripe_session_id IS NULL` — now prevented by the ME-046 fix), and the bank debit settles anyway, the CAS matches zero rows and the captured money is orphaned. With ME-046 shipped, `abandoned` cannot occur while an ACH debit is in-flight; this is a defence-in-depth note. Add `payment_status.eq.abandoned` to the CAS filter as a safety net, and add a test case. finding: lh-money-escrow@41c7848c9#2
- [ ] **Q1878 LOW Complete Checkout Session with a `canceled` PaymentIntent has no webhook handler to notify the poster (ME-046 non-blocking observation, lh-money-escrow R4 review of PR #3197, 2026-10-16).** `checkSessionStatus` allow-lists PI status `canceled` as `closed=true` (a genuine failure). If the PI reached `canceled` without triggering a `payment_intent.payment_failed` event (e.g. Stripe canceled it server-side), the poster never receives the "payment failed" notification and the job stays `failed` silently. Confirm whether Stripe fires `payment_intent.canceled` in this path; if so, add a minimal `paymentIntentCanceled` handler that calls `checkSessionStatus` and sends the not-posted notice under the same guard. GUARD when built: an edge test for the `payment_intent.canceled` webhook that asserts the notification fires. finding: lh-money-escrow@41c7848c9#3
- [ ] **Q1877 LOW After a card decline the job UI shows "failed" for days while ACH debit settles on the new checkout (ME-046 non-blocking observation, lh-money-escrow R4 review of PR #3197, 2026-10-16).** A poster whose card declined (job `payment_status='failed'`) re-opens Post a Job, loads the draft, and pays via ACH. The new checkout sets a fresh `stripe_session_id` on the job and the `useDraftCheckoutState` hook shows "open". But the job-card/status UI (where the poster sees their existing job) still renders `payment_status='failed'` for the 1-4 days the bank debit takes to settle, which is misleading. Consider showing "payment pending" when `stripe_session_id` is set and `payment_status` is `failed`/`unpaid`. GUARD when built: a UI state test that a job with `payment_status='failed'` and `stripe_session_id` set renders a "pending" label rather than "failed". finding: lh-money-escrow@41c7848c9#4
- [ ] **Q1876 MEDIUM Gift card is consumed at ACH checkout completion but not restored if the ACH debit bounces (ME-046 follow-up, Sentry bot finding on PR #3176, 2026-10-16).** `checkoutSessionCompleted` marks the gift card "redeemed" before detecting `isAsyncPayment`. If the ACH debit later bounces, `paymentIntentPaymentFailed` sets the job to "failed" but never calls `restore_gift_card_for_job`, leaving the donor's credit permanently spent on a job that was never funded. Only affects jobs that used a gift card for partial payment AND paid via ACH (US bank account). Fix: in `paymentIntentPaymentFailed`, when the job transitions to "failed" and `payment_method_types` includes "us_bank_account" (or `isAsyncPayment` is detectable), call `restore_gift_card_for_job` if a gift card was applied to the job. GUARD when built: a stripe-webhook test case with a gift-card-applied job and a `payment_intent.payment_failed` event that asserts the gift card is restored.

- [ ] **Q1882 LOW Declutter's designed refusals (wanted_rate_limited, wanted_open_limit, offer_rate_limited, a stale-cache feature_disabled, …) are reported to error_logs/Sentry at error severity by the app's QueryCache/MutationCache, though the member is shown plain words (lh-silent-failure review 2026-10-16, code read of src/lib/queryClient.ts:31-45).** Noise under the every-alert order; existing Declutter pattern, not new in the wanted-posts change. - [ ] **Q1886 MEDIUM GitHub has no workflow run of any workflow created between 2026-10-01 01:40Z and 2026-10-03 15:54Z, so about 2.5 days of run history was deleted (found by sec-c, 2026-10-17).** Measured by sec-c: `actions/runs?created=2026-10-02` returns total_count 0, against 1685 on 2026-09-30; runs from 2026-09-23 still exist. - [ ] **Q1887 LOW complete-signup's no-session one-shot check is read-then-write, so two overlapping no-session calls both pass (lh-authz-rls review of a02d0cd80, 2026-10-17; code read).** A Q1810 re-send arriving while the first request still runs, or a racing stranger, both pass the terms_accepted_at read, and the avatar is upserted to a fixed key before the UPDATE, so the loser can replace the stored photo. Fix: on the no-session path add `.is("terms_accepted_at", null)` to the profiles UPDATE and answer zero rows with "Profile already completed. Please log in."; also add guard cases for a `Bearer <anon key>` caller with getUser failing and for a non-object body (401, not 500), and correct the "with NO bio ... runs again" bullet in src/pages/auth/signup/retrySignupCompletion.ts (origin/wip/lander-rest-1017, Q1810) once both land; Q1888 is fixed for back-to-back re-sends by the one-shot rule (only this overlap remains). GUARD when built: src/test/edge/complete-signup-no-session-fields.test.ts. **PROGRESS 2026-10-17 (sec-b, branch ready/sec-b-1017-6 on -5 8533128e8): the atomic part and both guard cases are FIXED.** The no-session path now TAKES the one-shot slot with one conditional UPDATE (`terms_accepted_at = now() WHERE user_id = $1 AND terms_accepted_at IS NULL`, returning the row) right after the read check and BEFORE the photo upload or any write; zero rows answers "Profile already completed. Please log in.", so a racing call uploads and writes nothing. A holder that does not finish (any answer but 200, or a throw) RELEASES it (terms_accepted_at back to NULL, keyed on its own stamp), so the member's own retry is not locked out; the completion's UPDATE overwrites the stamp with the consent time. Residual, by design: an overlapping re-send that loses the claim is told "already finished" even if the holder then fails; the member then finishes through Log In. GUARD: src/test/edge/complete-signup-no-session-fields.test.ts, a live-row double where an UPDATE lands only if its WHERE matches at that moment (src/test/edge/mocks/completeSignupWrites.ts): Promise.allSettled of two no-session calls that both pass the read -> [200, 403], one upload, one consent row, one referral (red on 8533128e8: [200, 200]); refused-after-claim releases and the retry completes; the release is keyed on its own stamp (a completion that landed but whose answer failed is never cleared); `Bearer <anon key>` with getUser failing is the no-session path; non-object bodies (null, string, array, number) are 401 with no write. From the lh-authz-rls review of a8e881b8c (APPROVE WITH NOTES): the 18+ gate now runs BEFORE the photo upload, so a refused no-session call uploads nothing (red on a8e881b8c). 4 new @mutate killed; all 26 registered on complete-signup still killed. Not done (review note c, optional, pre-existing window): the no-session completion UPDATE could also require the claim stamp. LEFT: the "with NO bio ... runs again" bullet in src/pages/auth/signup/retrySignupCompletion.ts, which exists only on origin/wip/lander-rest-1017 (Q1810), so it cannot be edited here; correct it once Q1810 is on main (a re-send now meets "Profile already completed" whether or not a bio was saved; an overlapping one loses the claim). finding: lh-authz-rls@a02d0cd80#1; finding: lh-authz-rls@a02d0cd80#3 STATUS 2026-10-17: the batch1 commit "chore(signup): name Q1887 in the complete-signup comments" only renamed the placeholder Q1899 to Q1887 in comments; the item is unchanged (what remains is the retrySignupCompletion.ts bullet, still on origin/wip/lander-rest-1017).

- [~] **Q1895 MEDIUM poster-e2e's inbox carries a hidden unread thread from a journey, which turns three prod-audit checks red (run 39008504928).** Measured on prod 2026-10-17: one unread message from helper-e2e at 06:34:51Z on completed journey job c92f73d5 "[E2E DO NOT ACCEPT] J c0biv6", so HiddenUnreadBar shows. Separately decide whether the bar-to-list gap (space-y-2, 8px) should be --section-gap. BUILT 2026-10-17 (reds lane): prod-seed restoreNoHiddenUnread marks those messages read before each leg (seed jobs not live, seed senders, never a team thread). - [ ] **Q1900 MEDIUM On a cold visit to /messages the hidden-unread bar ("1 unread conversation isn't in Active — show all") arrives above the list after it loads, and loading-states measures row #0 60px -> 36px for both test accounts (loading-states-refresh 39008870955, 2026-10-17, measured by sec-c).** Cause, read from src/components/messages/HiddenUnreadBar.tsx and ConversationList.tsx: while loading, the bar's space is held only by the count this device cached last visit (`cachedHiddenUnread`); the harness opens a fresh browser context for each capture, so every capture is the documented first-visit shift (page-settle KNOWN in that file's header), and the loading-states inbox fixture leaves one unread conversation outside Active. Fix: decide which case is the real one and make the run match it. Either seed the device memory the way a returning member's device holds it (keep the first-visit shift as an accepted, screenshotted KNOWN), or keep the fixture's unread conversation in Active. Then re-run loading-states-refresh and record /messages #0 next to 60 -> 36. done-when: a loading-states-refresh run shows no /messages #0 breach.
- [ ] **Q1901 MEDIUM The loading-states request budget sits at one run's exact measurement, so the next run's noise fails it: perTest 2477 over its budget 2469 (loading-states-refresh 39008870955 step `request-budget.mjs --label loading-states`, 2026-10-17; 2469 is wip/reds-guards 6fd2dc8ee "budget at its measured value"; main still says 2116).** Busiest shapes in that run: `GET /storage/v1/object/public/avatars/:id/avatar.jpg` x197, `POST /rest/v1/rpc/feature_enabled_for` x119, `POST /rest/v1/rpc/get_my_pending_direct_offers` x114, `GET /rest/v1/profiles?select=*&user_id=eq.:id` x99. Fix: set the budget from at least two measured runs, with its headroom stated beside it, or cut the repeated reads (119 feature_enabled_for calls in one run is the first one to look at). done-when: two consecutive loading-states-refresh runs pass the budget step.
- [ ] **Q1917 LOW stalled-completion-reminder treats send-notification-email's 200 {success:false} (queue and direct send both failed) as sent (lh-silent-failure review of a94132b78, 2026-10-15).** send-notification-email answers HTTP 200 with success:false when it could neither queue nor send; stalled-completion-reminder/index.ts (~144) checks only res.ok, so a lost reminder email is reported clean. auto-expire-jobs was fixed for the same shape in the Q1817 work (it parses the body; skipped:true stays legitimate); _shared/refundNotice.ts already reads body.success. Also from the same review (#4): the Q1817 class guard (src/test/edge/auto-expire-jobs-cancel-email.test.ts) finds a notice by insertNotifications( within 16 lines of its title; match the notice const's name instead so a comment between them cannot reclassify it. Done when an edge test of stalled-completion-reminder with a 200 success:false answer records a defect, and the class guard matches by name. finding: lh-silent-failure@a94132b78#1
- [ ] **Q1904 LOW A cancel that runs in the seconds between a checkout funding its job and settleOnboardingFee refunding a duplicate $2 setup fee returns those $2 twice (lh-money-escrow review of ba10093ae, Q1675 follow-up; code read, narrow window, not seen on prod).** checkoutSessionCompleted sets payment_status 'escrow' (~958), calls instant-job-match, and only then runs settleOnboardingFee (~1227); a cancel_escrow or void-cancelled-payments in that gap refunds the full ceiling (which still holds the duplicate $2), then settleOnboardingFee refunds the $2 again out of the card fee. Fix: settleOnboardingFee skips its refund (and says so) when the charge's amount_refunded already covers the escrow ceiling, or the duplicate refund runs before the escrow flip. GUARD when built: a stripe-webhook edge test with a charge already refunded by a cancel. finding: lh-money-escrow@ba10093ae#3
- [ ] **Q1918 LOW A lost chargeback on a Checkout-taxed charge (a one-off job from create-payment, or a recurring visit paid through settleRecurringVisitCheckout) may leave its sales tax reported as collected (lh-money-escrow review of the Q1724 fix, 2026-10-14; not checked against Stripe).** Q1724 reverses the Tax API transaction an off-session visit charge records itself; charges taxed by Checkout `automatic_tax` are not handled on charge.dispute.closed (lost). Unverified whether Stripe Tax reverses a Checkout-taxed charge's tax on its own when a dispute is lost (its docs say it does not for the PaymentIntent-linked Tax API flow). Fix when confirmed: check in Stripe test mode or the Stripe Tax docs; if not automatic, reverse it on the lost branch of chargeDisputeClosed the way the visit Tax API reversal there does. GUARD when built: a stripe-webhook edge case with a lost dispute on a Checkout-taxed charge. finding: lh-money-escrow@803a6c258#3 (review of a superseded duplicate build of Q1724, bundle ~/.lh-backups/sec-a3-superseded-dupes-2026-10-15.bundle; main's Q1724 also reverses only the Tax API transaction a visit charge records itself)
- [ ] **Q1919 LOW A crew-seat accept or hire that runs at the same moment as a schedule-change accept can still double-book a Helpr (lh-authz-rls review of the Q1676 fix, 2026-10-15; code read on live definitions, not seen on prod).** Live, only respond_job_schedule_change takes the per-Helpr advisory lock `helpr_schedule:<helper>`; the crew-seat accept and hire paths do not, and its FOR SHARE on jobs rows does not stop a concurrent group_job_helpers insert, so a seat accepted in the same instant as a schedule change onto that time passes both checks. Older than Q1676 (not caused by it). Fix when built: the crew-seat accept / hire RPCs take the same advisory lock before their own clash check (or re-check after insert). GUARD when built: a PGlite race proof (two sessions, Promise.allSettled) that only one of the two lands. finding: lh-authz-rls@78c205c37#1
- [ ] **Q1902 LOW Three loose ends of the Q1491 job payout hold (lh-money-escrow review of the Q1491 rebuild 7133adc73, 2026-10-15).** (a) The hold is upserted before the escrow write's funded-job guard, so a delivery that turns out to be a duplicate funding (refunded in full by that branch) leaves a hold on a properly funded job: fail-safe, but it blocks a legitimate payout and the "underfunded" page is wrong. Fix: upsert with `.select("job_id")` (a row only when this delivery inserted it) and delete that hold in the duplicate-funding branch. (b) money-reconciliation excludes only Helpr holds, so a job-held payout_pending job is reported as payout_pending_stranded: load job_payout_holds and report it as held. (c) process-scheduled-payouts finds a job hold only at the claim, so every run repeats the account and transfer-group reads and rolls back the onboarding fee for each held job: call checkJobPayoutHold beside the Helpr check and skip with PAYOUT_HELD_CODE. Also weigh: void-cancelled-payments' two fee transfers send unlinked (no source_transaction) when latest_charge is missing; the trigger DETAIL says a held job pays nobody. GUARD when built: a stripe-webhook edge case of a duplicate-funded delivery (no hold left), a money-reconciliation case (held, not stranded) and a process-scheduled-payouts case (no Stripe read for a held job). finding: lh-money-escrow@7133adc73#3
- [ ] **Q1909 LOW No nightly check that a real (non-seed) row never holds a Stripe TEST-mode id (split from Q1761, 2026-10-15).** Q1761's fix keeps sandbox refund notices away from real people, but testEventScope still lets a test-mode event that names nobody through, on the assumption that only rows a test payment wrote hold its ids. Measured 2026-10-12 by the reviews (read-only SQL): 0 real jobs hold a cs_test_ session or a pre-go-live PaymentIntent. Nothing re-measures it. Fix: a nightly read-only check (prod-audit or the ops ledger) that counts non-seed jobs, recurring_visit_payments, payment_refunds, payout_transfers, gift_cards and tips (sendRefundNotice falls back to the last two for a recipient, and a test-mode full refund or dispute revokes a gift card by PaymentIntent; lh-money-escrow review of the Q1761 rebuild #4) holding a cs_test_/pi_ id minted before 2026-09-27 or a test Connect account, and pages on any. GUARD when built: a unit test of the check's query on a fixture with one such row (red) and none (green). finding: lh-money-escrow@db68f56ff#4
- [ ] **Q1920 LOW fixtureSchemaContract's literal scanner reads apostrophes in comments as string quotes, so one apostrophe added to a test file's comment can make it grade the whole file as one table row (found building Q1782 (2), 2026-10-15).** objectLiterals/balanced (src/test/helpers/schemaConstraints.ts) respects quotes but not comments: in src/test/edge/chargeRefundUpdated.test.ts an unbalanced brace in a header @mutate line plus one comment apostrophe turned a 9,537-character span (every test in the file) into one "recurring_visit_payments row" whose first status was a job's 'cancelled', a false red. Worked around in that file by rewording two comments; the scanner still has the hole (a parity flip can as easily HIDE a real bad fixture). Fix: run the source through blankComments (src/test/helpers/blankNonCode.ts) before objectLiterals. Done when: the scanner blanks comments and a test proves an apostrophe in a comment no longer changes which literals are graded.
- [ ] **Q1912 LOW Prove in Stripe TEST mode that a FULL visit tax reversal can be undone and the visit reversed again (lh-money-escrow + lh-silent-failure reviews of Q1782 (2), 2026-10-15).** Q1782 (2) reverses a refund that returns the whole labor + tax with mode full (the lead's rule), and a refund that later fails undoes its reversal with a full reversal of that reversal transaction. Stripe documents undoing a PARTIAL reversal this way, not a full one, and nothing says a re-issued refund's own reversal is then accepted on the original (it may be refused as an over-reversal: that pages, so it is not silent, but the tax on money really returned would stay reported). Also capture Stripe's real error for a reused reversal reference (type, code, param, message) so referenceTaken() in stripe-webhook/handlers/_visitTaxReversal.ts matches it exactly, and the real idempotency-in-use error. Not done in the build: no Stripe test key is on this Mac and the live account must not get test tax transactions. Done when: one test-mode run (tax calculation, createFromCalculation, full reversal, full reversal of that reversal, a second reversal on the original) is recorded with its ids, and the test fixtures use the captured errors (or the code switches to partial flat_amount everywhere if Stripe refuses the cycle). finding: lh-money-escrow@994f077b2#1
- [ ] **Q1907 LOW A TEST-mode gift the webhook refuses (not test-to-test) leaves its pre-registered gift_cards row pending forever, which reads as money in with no credit (lh-money-escrow review of Q1750, 2026-10-15).** stripe-webhook acknowledges and does not apply such a checkout.session.completed or .expired, so the row create-gift-card-checkout pre-registered (payment_status pending) is never closed out, the shape checkoutSessionExpired.ts says must be reported. Only reachable if the checkout rule is bypassed (the checkout refuses a non-test recipient before Stripe). Measured 2026-10-15: 0 gift_cards rows with a cs_test_ session. Fix: on that refusal mark the pending row expired (zero rows is fine), or have the reconciliation skip cs_test_ rows. Done when an edge test of the refused delivery asserts the row is closed. finding: lh-money-escrow@f5d7fee1d#3
- [ ] **Q1906 LOW A test-to-test gift picked by name is checked against the login email at checkout but against profiles.email in the webhook, so the two could disagree (lh-money-escrow review of Q1750, 2026-10-15).** create-gift-card-checkout resolves a picked recipient's email with auth.admin.getUserById; testGiftIsTestToTest (_shared/stripeMode.ts) finds the recipient by profiles.email. If those ever differ, a test charge completes and the gift is acknowledged but not applied. Measured 2026-10-15: 0 profiles whose profiles.email differs from auth.users.email (all 57 test accounts match). Fix: carry the resolved recipient id in the session metadata and have testGiftIsTestToTest require that id to be a test account whose profiles.email equals recipient_email. Done when an edge test with a mismatched email pins the outcome. finding: lh-money-escrow@f5d7fee1d#2
- [ ] **Q1905 LOW A LIVE gift card sent to a test account can be spent on a test-mode job, so the real buyer's money stays in the live balance while the job pays out in test mode (found building Q1750, 2026-10-15).** Lead decision for Q1750: a real buyer's gift checkout stays LIVE even when the recipient is a test account. Code read (not exercised): a wholly gift-funded job has no stored Checkout Session, so release-payout's jobMoneyMode decides by the job's parties, and a test poster with a test Helpr pays out on the TEST key. The live gift money then sits in the platform's live balance with no real person paid. Needs a decision: keep (it is the buyer's own choice and nothing real leaves), or have a gift carry its mode (gift_cards.stripe_session_id prefix) so a live gift funds only live jobs. Done when a test pins whichever rule is chosen.
- [ ] **Q1908 LOW A held tip or won-chargeback re-pay decides its Stripe mode from today's test-account enrolment, not from where the money sits (lh-money-escrow review of Q1753, 2026-10-15).** For a job with no cs_ prefix (gift-funded), an account enrolled or un-enrolled between the charge and the re-drive sends transfers.create to the other key; Stripe refuses it as a non-transient error, so the row goes 'failed' with a critical "pay by hand" page. No money is lost; someone is paged wrongly. Measured 2026-10-15: 0 open tip_hold_redrives rows and 0 open held chargeback_clawbacks rows. Fix: store the event's livemode on the row when the webhook writes it (a nullable stripe_mode column), re-drive on that mode (still checked against the payees), and fall back to clientForJob only when it is null. Done when an edge test with a stored mode that differs from today's enrolment pins it. finding: lh-money-escrow@886256a04#1
- [ ] **Q1903 LOW complete-signup files a phone doubt-check for a number it may never save, and its comments still describe a phone ban (lh-authz-rls review of Q1714, 2026-10-15).** complete-signup calls enforce_retained_ban with the phone from the request body before validation and before its other refusals; since Q1714 that files a doubt-check (no ban) even when the request then fails and the profile never holds that number. The call is redundant for the phone: the profiles UPDATE trigger (flag_possible_ban_evasion_by_phone) files it when complete-signup writes the phone. Its comments (~298-316, ~336-352) still describe a phone refusal (403) that can no longer happen. Fix: pass p_phone null (handle_new_user already checked the email) or drop the call, and rewrite the comments. Edge function change: redeploys complete-signup. Done when an edge test asserts the call sends no phone. Also from the same review: AdminBanEvasionReview.tsx (~125) comment still says "An auto-ban ('phone' at signup ...)". finding: lh-authz-rls@b37cf6db0#3
- [ ] **Q1911 LOW A split that loses to an admin's Clear test-mode records in the millisecond race says the wrong thing (lh-money-escrow review of the Q1764 fix f9a28da85, 2026-10-15).** execute-dispute-split's step-6 claim, now scoped away from the cleared marker, answers "this split is no longer executable — it may have already settled" when the marker is what refused it, and the in-claim re-read says "superseded, re-decided or already executed" without naming the clear. No money effect; only the admin's toast misleads, and only inside the race (the step-1 check already gives the exact words). Fix: re-read the marker before choosing the refusal text. GUARD when built: an edge case where the marker lands between step 1 and step 6, asserting the cleared wording. finding: lh-money-escrow@f9a28da85#4
- [ ] **Q1910 LOW A payout cron that read a test job just before an admin closed its dispute as a test can still send that job's TEST money and then page a false CRITICAL (lh-money-escrow review of the Q1769 close ea8471862, 2026-10-15).** process-scheduled-payouts (payout_pending) and auto-release-payment (escrow) select the job, then call checkUnsettledDispute; if rpc_close_test_mode_dispute commits between the two, the dispute reads executed (not blocked), the run transfers test-mode money, and its payment_status flip matches zero rows (the close moved the job to 'cancelled'), which pages "money moved but the job state did not flip" (process-scheduled-payouts ~:1859-1872). Test money only (every party is a test account, so the TEST key), and the window is the gap between the select and the gate. Fix: make checkUnsettledDispute treat a dispute closed as a test (execution_error starting "closed by an admin, Stripe test mode between test accounts") as blocked, or re-read payment_status under the gate. GUARD when built: an edge test where the close lands between the select and the gate, asserting no transfer and no page. finding: lh-money-escrow@ea8471862#3
- [ ] **Q1921 MEDIUM A gift-card-funded job whose payment answer is lost loses its photos on the retry and the poster is told "Couldn't start payment" (flagged in the 2026-10-17 hub handoff for haul-away v2; confirmed on main 7b4eceb3f by code read, not seen on prod).** src/pages/post-job/useJobSubmit.ts: when requestEscrowCheckout fails on the wire (outcomeUnknown) the job is kept for a retry (attempt.jobId, line 467-472). If the first call had already run redeem_gift_card to 'settled', the job is now payment_status 'escrow', so the retry's fee-model stamp in supabase/functions/create-payment/index.ts matches 0 rows and throws "This job's payment has already been processed". The client reads that as a server refusal and calls cleanupOrphanJob (line 729), which runs removeJobPhotos FIRST (the job row still exists, so the job-photos DELETE policy allows it) and then a job DELETE. Result: a paid job with its photos gone and an error toast. Fix: treat an already-funded answer for this attempt's own job as success (go to /payment-success), and never remove photos before the job row is confirmed deletable. The same retry path exists for haul-away v2's gift-paid jobs. GUARD when built: a test that drives startPayment with a lost first answer and an already-funded second answer and asserts no storage remove and no jobs delete.
- [ ] **Q1922 LOW Declutter haul-away: when Post a Job puts a failed booking's items back and some cannot go back, the seller is not told (lh-silent-failure review of the haul-away v2 delta, 2026-10-17; code read).** src/pages/post-job/useJobSubmit.ts ignores the failed count undoHaulAwayForJob returns at both undo call sites; failures reach only client-origin warnings, which never page. Fix: when failed > 0, add "Some items are still off sale; put them back from My listings." to the toast. GUARD when built: a haulAwayRelinkNoCleanup.test.tsx case where the undo reports 1 failed and the toast says so. finding: lh-silent-failure@bf3cd5730#3
- [ ] **Q1923 LOW Declutter haul-away prefill: a listing photo that fails to fetch (non-ok HTTP) is dropped with no report, and a malformed ?haulAway= opens an empty form that posts as a plain job with no link (lh-silent-failure review, 2026-10-17; code read of src/pages/post-job/useHaulAwayPrefill.ts:34 and :76).** Fix: report the HTTP status; toast and fall back to the entry screen when no ids parse. GUARD when built: useHaulAwayPrefill.test.tsx cases for both. finding: lh-silent-failure@bf3cd5730#6
- [ ] **Q1924 LOW Declutter haul-away: a full refund made from the Stripe Dashboard on a job whose Helpr was already paid ('released') never puts the items back on sale (lh-money-escrow review of the full-refund rule, 2026-10-17; code read).** chargeRefunded.ts leaves 'released' out of REFUND_CLOSABLE_PAYMENT_STATES and pages ops instead, so payment_status never becomes 'refunded' and trg_declutter_haul_away_job_refunded never fires; only the in-app admin refund (cancelled + refunded) and the payout-pending window relist. Fix: add to the ops runbook for that page: on a haul-away job, put the items back by hand (or extend the page with the haul-away item list). GUARD when built: NO-GUARD candidate (runbook text) unless the page is extended. finding: lh-money-escrow@bf3cd5730#3
- [ ] **Q1925 LOW Declutter notices link to /declutter?view=sales, a view that does not exist, so the member lands on Browse (found carrying the seller-out port into the stack, 2026-10-17; code read).** DeclutterHomeView.tsx:40 VIEWS = browse/mine/offers/cart/deliver/wanted and line 262 falls back to "browse" for anything else. The link is written by slice 4 (20261015042253, 3 sites, on main), 20261017055502 (1), 20261017055505 (3) and 20261017060455 (1). Fix: point each notice at the view that shows that member's order (the buyer's cart/purchases, the seller's sales), or add a 'sales' view. GUARD when built: a test that every '/declutter?view=' link in the newest migration bodies names a member of VIEWS.
- [ ] **Q1926 LOW On a late in-time Declutter payment (B41) a SELLER who blocked the buyer reads sold_elsewhere, so the platform pays the card fee, where the same block on an open checkout reads seller_backed_out and the seller pays (lh-money-escrow review of 905501cb2, finding 1, the part older than that commit; code read).** declutter_mark_order_paid path (b) in 20261017060455 (from 20261014224914) folds the seller's block into v_sale_ok and answers sold_elsewhere; path (c) picks seller_backed_out first. The buyer is refunded in full either way; only who absorbs the fee differs. The buyer-block half of the finding is fixed in the stack (seller_out judged on the items alone). Fix: pick (b)'s reason with (c)'s CASE, and decide the buyer-blocked case with the owner (a buyer_cancelled refund keeps the fee, which (b)'s "every cent" notice would then contradict). Same question for a BUYER who blocked a seller who is NOT out: (b) reads sold_elsewhere (platform pays), (c) reads buyer_cancelled (the card fee is kept) (same review, re-review note 2). GUARD when built: PGlite cases in src/test/pglite/declutterSellerOutPaysFee.pglite.mjs for a seller-blocked and a buyer-blocked late payment. finding: lh-money-escrow@905501cb2#1
- [ ] **Q1927 LOW A Declutter seller's card-fee debt and its 'Card fee owed' notice are booked before the Stripe refund runs, so a refund Stripe refuses for good still leaves the seller owing (lh-money-escrow review of 905501cb2, finding 3; code read).** declutter_record_refund writes declutter_seller_fee_debts inside the webhook's transaction, before executeRefund in supabase/functions/stripe-webhook/handlers/declutterCheckout.ts; when the refund gives up ("Settle it by hand") nothing cancels the debt. Same for seller_backed_out and the other seller-pays reasons. Fix: cancel or hold the debt when a refund gives up, and release it when a hand refund settles the row. GUARD when built: a PGlite case that a given-up refund leaves no live debt. finding: lh-money-escrow@905501cb2#3
- [ ] **Q1928 LOW Declutter seller-out fee debt: four gaps listed as open in docs/specs/declutter-auctions.md when owner answer 7 was built (2026-10-17).** (1) no way to waive a seller's fee debt when a ban or flag is overturned; (2) a debt booked from an ESTIMATED fee (Stripe balance transaction unreadable) is not marked as estimated; (3) a paid order whose seller goes out AFTER payment can still end as buyer_no_show / admin_no_fault, so the buyer bears the fee; (4) a seller who blocked the buyer and is also out is billed with no notice. Also for the owner: the debt is only taken from that seller's later Declutter payouts, so a banned seller's debt is usually never collected, and it is erased if the seller deletes the account (seller_id ON DELETE CASCADE). GUARD when built: a PGlite case per gap in src/test/pglite/declutterSellerOutPaysFee.pglite.mjs.
- [ ] **Q1929 MEDIUM Declutter delivery: row 40's "the buyer may raise the pay" when no Helpr takes a delivery is not built (declutter-delivery lane, 2026-10-16; spec B68).** Built instead: after 48 h with no Helpr the whole delivery fee is refunded and the order becomes a pickup at the seller's home (declutter_delivery_refund, reason no_helpr). Raising the pay needs a second payment on an already-paid order (a new Checkout session tied to the order, a helpr_tasks.pay_cents raise and the matching fee). OWNER QUESTION: build it, or keep refund-and-pickup. GUARD when built: a PGlite proof that a raise reopens the task at the new pay, its payout is the raised pay minus the fee, and an unpaid raise changes nothing.
- [ ] **Q1930 LOW Declutter delivery prices "road miles" as straight-line distance x 1.3, rounded up (declutter-delivery lane, 2026-10-16; spec B60).** The app has no server-side routing (drive time exists only on the device), so declutter_road_miles estimates. Across a river or a bayou the real drive can be far longer than 1.3x, so the Helpr is underpaid on those trips. Done when declutter_road_miles (one SQL function, the only place miles are measured) reads a routing service's distance, with the 1.3x estimate as the fallback, and a test pins a known river-crossing pair.
- [ ] **Q1931 MEDIUM Declutter delivery: a Helpr payout or a delivery-fee refund that gives up after 12 tries leaves its queue with at most one alert and no re-raise (declutter-delivery lane, 2026-10-16; code read).** declutter-money posts one Slack alert on the try that reaches 12 for a Helpr payout, and a delivery-fee refund that THROWS records only a cron defect; after that helpr_task_payouts_due / declutter_delivery_refunds_due skip the row forever, while the buyer was told "your delivery fee is coming back". Seller refunds already have declutter_refund_gave_up_check (20261017055500). Two more parts of the same gap (lh-money-escrow and lh-silent-failure re-reviews of db0ce9b8b): a delivery-fee refund ops sends BY HAND is never recorded (declutter_record_hand_refund marks only declutter_refunds rows), so the stricter refunds_recorded_cents never counts it and a seller or Helpr payout on that charge gives up as "not whole"; and a Helpr payout that gave up (failed, attempts >= 12) blocks the Helpr's account deletion forever (accountPurge treats every failed payout as owed). Done when both queues have the same gave-up check (one error_logs ledger row per row per day while unsettled, plus a buyer / Helpr notice), proven red-before in a PGlite proof. finding: lh-silent-failure@88fa498a4#4
- [ ] **Q1932 MEDIUM Declutter delivery: a task the Helpr picked up never times out (declutter-delivery lane, 2026-10-16; code read of helpr_task_sweep).** The sweep handles open, accepted and delivered tasks, never picked_up, and both handover-sweep loops skip delivery orders, so a Helpr who has the item and disappears leaves the order paid (seller and Helpr money held) until the buyer reports a problem. Done when picked_up past pickup_slot_start + 12 h opens a declutter_problems row for an admin and pages ops (PGlite proof red-before). finding: lh-authz-rls@88fa498a4#5
- [ ] **Q1933 MEDIUM Declutter delivery: exact addresses outlive the buyer's and seller's account deletion (declutter-delivery lane, 2026-10-16; code read).** helpr_tasks.pickup_address / dropoff_address and their points, and declutter_order_deliveries.dropoff_*, keep the street addresses after customer_id / pickup_contact_id are SET NULL (CLAUDE.md "Deletion anonymises"). Done when those columns are nullable and purge_user_data blanks them on closed tasks and orders of the deleted member (proof in the account-deletion PGlite suite). finding: lh-authz-rls@88fa498a4#7
- [ ] **Q1934 LOW Declutter delivery: a damaged-in-delivery refund waits until the seller's payout has gone, which can be never (declutter-delivery lane, 2026-10-16; code read of declutter_refunds_due).** A seller on a payout hold, without a payout account, or whose payout gives up, keeps the buyer's full refund out of the queue though the buyer was told it is coming. Done when the refund is released after N days or once the seller payout fails or is held (alerting ops), with a PGlite proof. finding: lh-money-escrow@88fa498a4#3
- [ ] **Q1935 LOW Declutter delivery: after the delivery fee is refunded on its own, a later seller-fault or no-fault refund still takes the card fee on the WHOLE payment (declutter-delivery lane, 2026-10-16; code read of declutter_close_paid_order).** It passes o.card_fee_cents (the fee on the total including the delivery fee) while refunding only total minus the reserved delivery fee, so the seller's debt (or the kept fee) includes card cost on money already given back. Done when the fee is prorated to (total - reserved) / total and refundsKeepTheCardFee pins it. finding: lh-money-escrow@88fa498a4#6
- [ ] **Q1936 MEDIUM Declutter Add a Helpr: a refunded order leaves its paid setup job running and tells the buyer nothing about it. OWNER DECIDED 2026-10-17 (spec A10): on a SELLER-fault refund (fee_policy full_seller_pays_fee) the setup job is cancelled with no cancellation fee, the card fee is returned, and the seller owes it as a Declutter fee debt; a condition for declutter_add_helpr leaving testers. THE SELLER-FAULT CASE IS BUILT (2026-10-17, add-a-helpr v2: trg_declutter_refund_cancels_setup_jobs + void-cancelled-payments reads seller_fault_refund_id + declutter_setup_job_fee_debt; guards src/test/pglite/declutterAddAHelpr.pglite.mjs A1-A10 and src/test/edge/void-cancelled-payments.declutterSellerFault.test.ts); STILL OPEN: the buyer's notice on every other refund (declutter-add-a-helpr-v2 lane, 2026-10-17; code read: no refund path in supabase/migrations reads declutter_order_jobs, 20261017073146 is its only reader).** When a Declutter order with a linked, paid Helpr job (assemble / wall-mount / set up) is refunded (no-show, problem decided for the buyer, no-fault close), the job stays open and funded, and the order card's Add a Helpr panel disappears (declutter_setup_helpr answers item_not_found for a refunded order), so the buyer may pay a Helpr to set up an item they no longer have. The link moves no money, so nothing is charged twice, but nothing tells the buyer. Done when every path that sets declutter_orders.status = 'refunded' sends the buyer a notice naming each linked, funded, not-cancelled job with a link to cancel it under the normal cancel rules (never auto-cancelled: the Helpr may already be booked), proven red-before in a PGlite proof on the real Declutter schema. finding: lh-money-escrow@a98e0049d6#3
- [ ] **Q1937 MEDIUM Declutter Add a Helpr A10: the seller's card-fee debt for the buyer's setup jobs has no cap per order (OWNER DECISION).** declutter_setup_job_fee_debt caps each job at its own charge, but an order can link any number of jobs of any category and price, so a $5 sale with five $500 linked jobs bills the seller about $74 of card fees. A buyer-reported seller_no_show (no rebuttal) still cancels and bills uncommitted funded jobs, so the buyer's word alone can charge an innocent seller the card fees (options: on seller_no_show cancel but bill only after an admin confirms through declutter_admin_apply_seller_fault, or the per-order cap covers it; lh-authz-rls re-review of 24b71630a). Decide before the switch widens past testers. Done when the owner picks a rule (cap per refund at the order total, a fixed ceiling, or only assembly/handyman jobs linked within N hours) and a PGlite case proves it. finding: lh-authz-rls@c57d948d0#2

- [ ] **Q1962 MEDIUM Declutter haul-away: tell the seller (bell + push) on the day a listing can be hauled away (14 days unsold, docs/specs/declutter-haul-away.md H1), and put items back on sale when a haul-away was abandoned.** CONDITION FOR GOING LIVE: the declutter_haul_away switch stays testers-only until the put-back-on-sale sweep below is live on prod (re-review 2026-10-17, finding 4: an item whose haul-away checkout was abandoned or failed stays off sale (ended) until then); raised LOW -> MEDIUM for that. The haul-away itself is built behind the testers-only switch declutter_haul_away (migration 20261017070827); today the seller only finds it on My listings, and an item whose haul-away was posted but never paid stays off sale until the seller taps "Put back on sale" (lh-money-escrow review 2026-10-16 #3). Needs a daily sweep that notifies once per item (a notified-at mark) through declutter_notify, respecting notification preferences, and restores links whose job holds nothing (declutter_haul_job_hold = none) after 7 days. Also: a haul-away job left open with payment_status 'failed' (declined card) and no session can't be deleted by its poster (the jobs DELETE policy allows only unpaid-without-session or abandoned) and void-cancelled-payments only sweeps 'unpaid', so its items stay off sale and My listings says it can't be removed (lh-money-escrow re-review 2026-10-16 #1, lh-authz-rls re-review A(b)); the sweep should move it to 'abandoned' (money-lane files, needs an lh-money-escrow review). done-when: a tester's listing that reaches 14 days unsold gets one bell row on prod and no second one the next day, and an abandoned haul-away's item is back on sale after the sweep. finding: lh-money-escrow@9a6078c03#3
- [ ] **Q1963 MEDIUM Tapping Post while on /post-job?declutterOrder= (or ?haulAway=) keeps the pre-filled form on screen but drops the link, so the job can be saved as a plain draft and posted and paid with no link to the Declutter order (lh-money-escrow + lh-silent-failure review of the lander's Declutter-train merge 9c855c737, 2026-10-17; code read, not driven).** MobileNav.tsx:657 and DesktopSidebarNav.tsx:232 navigate("/post-job"); the route is not keyed (App.tsx:245), so PostJob stays mounted. usePostJobForm recomputes noDraft and declutterOrderId from the live URL each render: noDraft turns false (the 2 s autosave in useJobFormEffects stores the Add a Helpr form as an ordinary draft) and declutterOrderId turns null (the job posts with no linkSetupJobOrTell), so a seller-fault refund of the order (declutter_refund_cancels_setup_jobs, 20261017073146) would not cancel or refund that setup job. A haul-away keeps its itemIds in state, so only its draft gate is hit. Fix: decide the intent once per mount (useState initialiser, as useHaulAwayPrefill keeps itemIds) and remount or reset the form when ?declutterOrder=/?haulAway= disappears (key the form subtree on them). GUARD when built: a usePostJobForm test that drops the param mid-mount and asserts no draft write and the order link kept. finding: lh-money-escrow@9c855c737#2
- [ ] **Q1964 LOW No test runs a refused adminDecideDeliveryDamage through the admin error copy it now loads on demand (lh-silent-failure review of 9c855c737, 2026-10-17).** DeclutterProblemsCard.test.tsx mocks the handover functions, so the lazy loadAdminRpcErrorCopy path in src/lib/declutter/handover.ts (refusal -> admin copy, chunk failure -> DECLUTTER_ERROR_COPY fallback) rests on a code read only. GUARD when built: a handover.test case with a refused RPC that asserts the admin sentence, and one with the admin chunk failing that asserts the fallback sentence. finding: lh-silent-failure@9c855c737#9
- [ ] **Q1965 MEDIUM When void-cancelled-payments' refunded-past-kept-fees skip trips on a Declutter seller-fault setup job, the page does not say it is a seller fault and the seller's card-fee debt is never billed (lh-money-escrow review of the train's rebase resolution, 2026-10-17; code read).** supabase/functions/void-cancelled-payments/index.ts:2116-2131 pages "Cancelled job's charge was already refunded past what the cancel keeps" and `continue`s before the A10 fee-debt block (:2286-2312), so whoever reconciles it by hand must also bill the seller, and nothing tells them to. Fix: add `seller_fault: true` (and the debt the seller owes) to the page's fields, or bill the debt before the skip. GUARD when built: a void-cancelled-payments.declutterSellerFault case with latest_charge.amount_refunded past the ceiling asserting the page names the seller fault. finding: lh-money-escrow@3d92f1362#2
- [ ] **Q1966 LOW No test runs a Declutter seller-fault cancel on a charge that already had a partial refund (lh-money-escrow review of the train's rebase resolution, 2026-10-17).** src/test/edge/void-cancelled-payments.declutterSellerFault.test.ts never sets latest_charge.amount_refunded, so the A10 + Q1675 combination (alreadyRefundedCents with sellerFault ? 0) rests on the reviewer's arithmetic (captured 10549, earlier 3000, setup 200 -> refund 7349). GUARD when built: that case, asserting the refund amount and that the seller debt is still billed. finding: lh-money-escrow@3d92f1362#1
- [ ] **Q1967 LOW Post a Job's lazy haul-away module (haulAwayLib in useJobSubmit) can start the stale-chunk recovery reload mid-submit, between the job row and the checkout redirect (lh-money-escrow review of 6ef39799d #3a, 2026-10-17; code read).** A tab left open across a deploy: vite:preloadError -> recoverFromChunkError reloads the page while startPayment runs; the Add a Helpr link now loads through backgroundImport for this reason, the haul-away link does not. No money moves without a completed checkout, but the seller is left with an unpaid hidden job and a reload. Fix: load haulAwayLib through backgroundImport, or warm it when the form mounts with ?haulAway=. GUARD when built: declutterHaulAway asserts the backgroundImport wrapper. finding: lh-money-escrow@6ef39799d#3
- [ ] **Q?applegrace LOW An Apple member in Billing Grace Period is graded as lapsed (lh-money-escrow review of 54e7b92b7, 2026-10-17; pre-existing).** apple-app-store-notifications says "a grace period shows up as an expiresDate still in the future", but per Apple's App Store Server API docs the grace window is JWSRenewalInfo.gracePeriodExpiresDate and the transaction's expiresDate is not extended, so a member in grace (subscription status 4) has the tier cleared and gets it back only if the renewal goes through. Only matters if Billing Grace Period is switched on in App Store Connect (owner setting, not measured). Prod 2026-10-17: 0 Apple-anchored profiles. Fix: fetchLatestSubscriptionTransaction (_shared/appleAppStore.ts, Q1860) already receives status and signedRenewalInfo; return them, and treat status 4 with gracePeriodExpiresDate > now as entitled until gracePeriodExpiresDate. GUARD when built: an edge case in src/test/edge/apple-app-store-notifications.test.ts where status 4 with a future gracePeriodExpiresDate keeps the tier. finding: lh-money-escrow@54e7b92b7#2
- [ ] **Q?escrow-notice-cancelled LOW The poster of a CANCELLED job whose bank debit settles late is told "Payment secured in escrow ... will release after the job is completed" before the refund notice (lh-money-escrow review of af0fbedd6, nit 1; pre-existing).** paymentIntentSucceeded promotes a cancelled unfunded job to escrow on purpose (void-cancelled-payments Part A refunds it within the hour), but notify_on_payment_escrowed (AFTER UPDATE on jobs, prod 2026-10-17) has no status guard, so the poster first reads that their money is held for a job they cancelled. Was already true for cancelled+unpaid jobs before Q1879. Prod 2026-10-17: 0 unpaid/failed/abandoned jobs hold a PaymentIntent. Fix: skip the escrow notice when NEW.status is 'cancelled' or 'completed' (migration, CREATE OR REPLACE, grants restated). GUARD when built: a PGlite or SQL-reading test that the escrow notice is not inserted for a cancelled job. finding: lh-money-escrow@af0fbedd6#1
- [ ] **Q?ach-settled-closed-silent LOW A bank payment that settles on a job already refunded/cancelled/cancelling is acked with no alert (lh-money-escrow review of af0fbedd6, nit 2; pre-existing).** paymentIntentSucceeded only acts when the job is null/unpaid/failed/abandoned; any other payment_status falls through silently, and a settled ACH debit on a job whose payment_status is 'refunded', 'cancelled' or 'cancelling' is captured money with no escrow to hold it. Prod 2026-10-17: no such rows with a PaymentIntent. Fix: page money_at_risk when the job's payment_status is outside escrow/payout_pending/released and the promotable set (a redelivery for a card payment that was later refunded must not page: key it on the PI being an ACH one or on the refund ledger). GUARD when built: a stripe-webhook payment_intent.succeeded case on a 'refunded' job that asserts the page. finding: lh-money-escrow@af0fbedd6#2
